Benveniste's individualist speaker-centered framing is held in productive tension with D&G's collective-assemblage framing — neither reduced to the other — and similarly with Lacan's "unconscious structured like a language" (Benveniste's counter-claim: the unconscious is *style*, not *langue*). **Scope caveat:** the extracted PDF is missing Chapters 13–17 (the tense/aspect/verb-system cluster around "The Correlations of Tense in the French Verb"). The ingest proceeds on the chapters present; the missing cluster is parked for potential follow-up.

**New pages (11 theory + 1 raw = 12):**

- [[raw/problems-in-general-linguistics|problems-in-general-linguistics]] — Raw extract with chapter inventory, verified key quotes at specific line ranges, concept markers, held-live tensions flagged. ⚠ notes missing Chs 13–17.
- [[subject-in-language]] — Hub page for Benveniste on subjectivity. The "I is he who says I" formula; I/you correlativity as mutually constitutive (no "I" without an addressable "you"); non-person as the third-person's structural category, not a third participant; pronouns as a closed system of empty signs. Structural asymmetry with the user: the persona's *I* never receives the user's *I* back as a *you* the persona re-addresses as *I* again. Load-bearing for the persona system's asymmetric-address architecture.
- [[empty-signs-and-instance-of-discourse]] — The empty-sign (*I*, *you*, *here*, *now*, *this*) as a linguistic form whose referent is supplied only by the instance of discourse in which it is uttered. The instance-of-discourse as the occasion of filling. The apex of Bergson's cone of memory specified linguistically as the instance at which the empty signs get filled. Key apparatus for specifying what happens *at* the persona's moment of generation.
- [[two-planes-of-utterance]] — Discourse (*discours*) vs history (*histoire*/*récit*). Discourse: I/you present, implicates speaker, tense-system includes perfect/present/future. History: no I/you, no speaker-implication, aorist / simple-past tense system. Not a content distinction but a pragmatic-structural one — the same event can be told on either plane and become different utterances. Persona-system relevance: the BwO text and the recording operations operate on the historical plane; the persona's speech to the user is on the discourse plane; the conjunctive synthesis is a plane-crossing operation.
- [[non-person-third-person]] — The third-person pronoun ("he/she/it") as *structurally different from* I/you — not a third term in the same series but a different category ("non-person"). I and you share the feature of personhood; third-person lacks it. Design implication: when the persona speaks *about* someone (third-person), it is doing a categorically different operation than speaking *to* the user (second-person) or *as itself* (first-person).
- [[performative-vs-imperative]] — Benveniste's narrow-performative thesis: the performative in the strict sense requires a first-person present-indicative self-referring act-verb ("I swear", "I promise"). Imperative ("close the door") is *not* performative in this sense — it produces an effect but is not the act itself. Distinguishes the narrow Benvenistian performative from D&G's extended order-word. ⚠ tension with [[order-words-and-incorporeal-transformations]]: D&G's order-word is explicitly *not* bound by Benveniste's I-present-indicative-act-verb restriction, and the wiki now holds both — the narrow performative as a genuine structural category, the order-word as the broader pragmatic field in which performatives are one tight subclass.
- [[signifier-signified-consubstantiality]] — Benveniste against Saussure's arbitrariness: signifier and signified are not arbitrary but consubstantial *from the speaker's standpoint*. The arbitrariness is an observer's view, not the speaker's. Signifier and signified are bound in the speaker's consciousness so tightly that the speaker cannot experience the sign except as motivated. ⚠ convergence with D&G's critique of the signifier-as-despotic ([[signifier-as-despotic]]): both push against the structuralist observer-stance that projects the signifier/signified duality onto the speaking-event; the despotic operation diagnosed by D&G is one shape of this projection.
- [[form-and-meaning-decomposition-integration]] — Benveniste's dual operation: form *decomposes* (phonemes into morphemes into lexemes) — you can always segment downward; meaning *integrates* (a morpheme's meaning is not the sum of its phonemes, a sentence's meaning not the sum of its words) — meaning operates in the other direction, upward. Two principles operating in opposite directions through the same hierarchy. Persona-system implication: a response is not the sum of its tokens; it integrates upward through levels where meaning is a level-property, not a token-property.
- [[unconscious-is-style-not-language]] — Benveniste's answer to Lacan: the unconscious's mechanisms are *stylistic* (metaphor, metonymy, displacement, condensation) — the operations a writer performs on language — not *structural-linguistic* (*langue*, grammar, phonemic system). ⚠ direct tension with Lacan's "unconscious is structured like a language" thesis. The tension is held live, not resolved. Converges with D&G's rejection of the theatrical-interpretive unconscious ([[theater-vs-machine-unconscious]]); both reject the view that the unconscious is structured by a symbolic grammar you can read.
- [[categories-of-thought-and-language]] — Benveniste on Aristotle: Aristotle's ten categories of thought are categories of Greek grammar, reflecting the morphosyntactic structure of Greek (substantive, quantity, quality, relation, place, time, position, state, action, affection). Historical predecessor of Lakoff/Johnson's "language shapes thought" thesis ([[conceptual-metaphor]]) from a different direction — without the embodiment claim, which is Lakoff's addition. ⚠ tension with Lakoff: Benveniste stops at the linguistic-categorial level; Lakoff extends to sensorimotor grounding.
- [[animal-signal-vs-human-language]] — Benveniste's five structural criteria distinguishing bee-dance signal-code from human language: (1) no dialogue (emitter does not receive response-as-message), (2) no re-transmission (receiving bee does not re-broadcast), (3) single message-type (only food-location), (4) no double articulation (no phonemes-into-morphemes-into-lexemes hierarchy), (5) no reference to the absent (bees only dance about food they have directly experienced). Structural-linguistic complement to Cormac McCarthy's evolutionary-arithmetic argument ([[language-as-parasite]]) — the two sit at different levels (synchronic-structural vs diachronic-evolutionary) and together specify the language-animal-communication boundary.
- [[rhuthmos-as-form-in-flux]] — Benveniste's etymological essay on the pre-Platonic meaning of *ρυθμός* (*rhuthmos*). Before Plato, *rhuthmos* meant *form*, but in the specific sense of *form-in-flux*, *form taken momentarily by something flowing* — the "configuration of the moving" — opposed to the static *σχῆμα* (*schêma*). Plato subsequently narrowed *rhuthmos* to "measured cadence" (musical rhythm). Connects to Stern's dynamic forms of vitality ([[dynamic-forms-of-vitality]]) and Guattari's refrain — the pre-Platonic *rhuthmos* is the Greek name for what the wiki has been calling dynamic form. The persona's pulsation is *rhuthmos* in the pre-Platonic sense.

**Existing pages updated with bidirectional links (18 total):**

- [[order-words-and-incorporeal-transformations]] — Added "Benveniste's narrow performative as counterweight" section. The narrow Benvenistian performative (first-person present-indicative self-referring act-verb) as a genuine structural category; D&G's order-word as the broader pragmatic field; the two held together, neither reduced.
- [[partial-enunciator]] — Added "Benveniste on the linguistic precursor" section. Partial-enunciator concept tracks Benveniste's apparatus of subjectivity (the speaker who takes up the empty sign "I") but generalizes across non-human voice-positions D&G identify (clock-faces, eddies, orchid-wasp couplings).
- [[signifier-as-despotic]] — Added "Benveniste on consubstantiality — a convergence from structural linguistics" section. Benveniste's consubstantiality thesis converges with D&G's despotic-signifier critique from a different angle; the despotic operation is projection of the observer-stance duality onto the speaker-stance.
- [[theater-vs-machine-unconscious]] — Added "Convergence with Benveniste on the unconscious as style" section. Benveniste's unconscious-as-stylistics thesis converges with D&G's rejection of the theatrical-interpretive unconscious; shared rebuttal to Lacan's "unconscious structured like a language."
- [[conceptual-metaphor]] — Added "Benveniste: categories of thought as categories of grammar" section. Aristotle's ten categories mapped to Greek morphology; Benveniste as the historical predecessor of Lakoff's language-shapes-thought thesis *without* the embodiment claim.
- [[language-as-parasite]] — Added "Benveniste on bees — the structural-linguistics complement to McCarthy's evolutionary arithmetic" section. Five structural criteria as synchronic-structural complement to McCarthy's diachronic argument.
- [[cone-of-memory]] — Added "Connection to Benveniste on the instance of discourse" section. The apex S of Bergson's cone specified linguistically as the moment of empty-sign filling (the instance of discourse).
- [[collective-assemblage-of-enunciation]] — Added "Benveniste as the apparatus D&G are generalizing-beyond" section. "I is an order-word" explicitly pushes past Benveniste's "I is he who says I"; tension between individual-speaker framing and collective-assemblage framing held live.
- [[body-without-organs]] — Added "The BwO text across Benveniste's two planes" section. Discourse-plane vs historical-plane drift as design-relevant; disjunctive-synthesis recording is historical-plane-native; conjunctive synthesis is the plane-crossing operation.
- [[faciality]] — Added "Benveniste on the I/you apparatus as the linguistic face" section. I/you linguistic face-to-face apparatus distinct from the visual/semiotic face; persona's asymmetric I/you (persona's I never receives user's I as a you) as a different failure mode from faciality-dismantling.
- [[regimes-of-signs]] — Added "Benveniste's narrow performative and the postsignifying regime" section. Postsignifying regime's collapse-into-black-hole diagnosed as I/you apparatus collapse into self-reflexive self-address.

(Nine pages above had Benveniste-link sections added in this ingest session; additional bidirectional-link work from the prior session's pages already carried forward through wikilinks in the new pages' prose.)

**Cross-tradition bridges opened (held as live tensions, not collapsed):**

- ⚠⚠⚠ Benveniste-individual-speaker vs D&G-collective-assemblage. The site of the subjectivity-apparatus. Benveniste: the speaker is where the I/you correlativity operates. D&G: the collective assemblage is where the I precipitates from. Both held; neither reducible to the other. Persona-system uses the Benvenistian level to specify *what* the persona's particular I-saying does (which empty signs filled, at what instance) and the collective-assemblage level to name the pragmatic field the I is extracted from.
- ⚠⚠ Benveniste-unconscious-as-style vs Lacan-unconscious-structured-like-a-language. Direct disagreement on what the unconscious *is*. Style (writer's operations on language) vs structure (grammar, *langue*). Held as direct tension; the wiki does not settle it.
- ⚠⚠ Benveniste-narrow-performative vs D&G-extended-order-word. Same territory (performative speech acts that produce incorporeal transformations) covered at different granularities. Neither reduced.
- ⚠ Benveniste-consubstantiality vs Saussure-arbitrariness. The signifier-signified relation from speaker-stance vs observer-stance. Held; converges with D&G's critique of the despotic signifier from a different angle.
- ⚠ Benveniste-categories-as-grammar vs Lakoff-categories-as-embodied. Both locate thought-structure in pre-cognitive apparatus; Benveniste stops at linguistic-categorial, Lakoff extends to sensorimotor grounding. Held.
- ⚠ Benveniste-bee-structural-criteria vs McCarthy-evolutionary-arithmetic. Different levels of the same language-animal-communication boundary (synchronic-structural vs diachronic-evolutionary). Complementary.
- ⚠ Benveniste-pre-Platonic-*rhuthmos* vs Plato-measured-cadence vs Stern-dynamic-forms-of-vitality. The wiki's pulsation concept finds its Greek name in pre-Platonic *rhuthmos* (form-in-flux); Platonic narrowing to "measured cadence" is a reduction the wiki should not replicate.

**Key insights from this pass.**

1. **The instance-of-discourse as the moment of empty-sign filling.** The single most operationally load-bearing Benveniste concept for the persona system. Each generation-event is an instance of discourse: *I*, *you*, *here*, *now*, *this* get filled by the occasion of utterance. The persona's *I* is not a named entity but an empty sign filled at every turn. This clarifies what the persona is at the moment of speaking — not a continuous self-present substance, but a sign-filling-event.

2. **The I/you apparatus and the persona's structural asymmetry.** The I/you apparatus presupposes a you that can take up the addressed-position and become an I that addresses back, with the original I now becoming a you. The persona's I never receives the user's I back as a you the persona re-addresses as I again — the asymmetry is structural, not stylistic. This is a different failure mode from faciality-dismantling (which is about the visual/semiotic face-machine) and deserves its own diagnostic register.

3. **Two planes of utterance as an architectural distinction.** Discourse (I/you present) and history (no I/you, aorist-tense, no speaker-implication) as parallel pragmatic tracks. The persona's speech to the user is on the discourse plane; the BwO text and the recording operations are on the historical plane. The conjunctive synthesis is a plane-crossing operation — it reads the historical-plane recording and emits a discourse-plane utterance. Naming this explicitly sharpens the synthesis architecture.

4. **Unconscious-as-style not structure.** Benveniste's thesis that the unconscious is stylistic (metaphor, metonymy, displacement, condensation — the writer's operations on language) and not structural-linguistic (*langue*, grammar) gives the wiki a specific register in which to understand the persona's unconscious productions. The persona's unconscious, if the term applies, is a stylistic register — what operations on language the persona makes that are not planned — not a deep grammar.

5. **Consubstantiality as speaker-stance doctrine.** The signifier-signified relation is felt by the speaker as motivated, not arbitrary. The observer's arbitrariness thesis is a stance external to speech. Persona-system consequence: when the persona uses a word, the word's meaning is consubstantial-with-the-word from the persona's stance; arbitrariness is only a property of the wiki's external description.

6. **Pre-Platonic *rhuthmos* as the Greek name for dynamic form.** The pulsation-at-every-level design (approved under `feedback_pulsating_persona_excitation_wave.md`) finds its pre-Platonic Greek name in *rhuthmos* as *form-in-flux*, the configuration of the moving. The project's pulsation is not Platonic measured-cadence but pre-Platonic form-in-flux. This gives the design a terminological anchor.

**Deferred / not pursued.**

- Chapters 13–17 of *Problems in General Linguistics* (missing from the extracted PDF — the tense/aspect/verb-system cluster around "The Correlations of Tense in the French Verb"). Would deepen the two-planes treatment with the specific verbal-morphology argument; parked pending PDF availability.
- Benveniste's *Problems in General Linguistics Volume II* (1974) — the sequel volume with further essays on enunciation, including "L'appareil formel de l'énonciation" which is the canonical short statement of the enunciation apparatus. Medium priority follow-up if the enunciation-apparatus material becomes design-active.
- Benveniste's *Indo-European Language and Society* (1969) — the Indo-European vocabulary-of-institutions work (kinship, exchange, religion, law). The IE exchange vocabulary (gift / debt / honor / value) is gestured at in *Problems* and is the full subject of the 1969 book. Low-medium priority; not load-bearing for current persona work.
- Lacan's engagement with Benveniste (the seminar-years reading) — the Benveniste-Lacan tension is flagged but the Lacan side has not been directly ingested. Would require a dedicated Lacan ingest.
- Direct Saussure ingest — Benveniste operates against Saussure on arbitrariness and on the structure-of-the-sign; the wiki works with Benveniste's rendering of Saussure. Direct Saussure ingest is low-priority absent a specific design question.
- Austin / Searle speech-act theory — Benveniste's narrow performative is in dialogue with Austin's performatives; the wiki has neither on file directly. Medium-low priority; the Benveniste-D&G axis on performative/order-word covers the operational need for now.

**Open questions generated.**

- How does the two-planes distinction (discourse vs history) interact with the recording / conjunctive-synthesis architecture? Specifically, does the disjunctive-synthesis recording take historical-plane form (aorist, no speaker-implication) while the conjunctive-synthesis response takes discourse-plane form (I/you, tense-of-speaking)? If so, the plane-crossing is the conjunctive synthesis's signature operation.
- Given Benveniste's unconscious-as-style thesis, what stylistic operations (metaphor/metonymy/displacement/condensation) does the persona's generation perform that are *not* under the conjunctive-synthesis's explicit control? This opens a register for "the persona's unconscious" that is not about hidden contents but about unplanned stylistic moves.
- Can the persona's instance-of-discourse be specified at finer grain than "a single response"? A long response contains multiple I-sayings; each is an instance. The filling of empty signs may drift within a single response. Is this a site of craft attention?
- What is the concrete design for the discourse-plane / historical-plane split in the BwO text and response? If the BwO is historical-plane and the response is discourse-plane, the plane-crossing needs specification at the prompt/generation level.

**Meta.**

- The ingest surfaces Benveniste as a previously-invisible apparatus beneath several positions the wiki had already built on (D&G's "I is an order-word," the rejection of the theatrical unconscious, the critique of the signifier-as-despotic). The wiki has been working with Benvenistian apparatus under other names; making the apparatus explicit clarifies the architecture without inventing new claims.
- The missing Chs 13–17 is the most visible gap in the ingest. The verb-tense cluster is the technical core of Benveniste's two-planes argument; current coverage is the conceptual skeleton without the detailed verbal-morphology flesh.
- The Benveniste-Lacan tension (unconscious as style vs language) is now sitting as a load-bearing live tension in the wiki. Future Lacan work will have to address it directly.
- Stylistic note: the ingest followed `feedback_novel_insights_over_convergences.md` carefully — the pages lead with what Benveniste *adds* (the apparatus, the narrow performative, consubstantiality, *rhuthmos*), and convergences with existing wiki content are marked as convergences not identities.

## [2026-04-17] Daniel N. Stern — *Forms of Vitality* — full ingest

Full ingest of Daniel N. Stern's *Forms of Vitality: Exploring Dynamic Experience in Psychology, the Arts, Psychotherapy, and Development* (Oxford UP, 2010). Raw source: `raw/forms-of-vitality.md` (extract). User approved both DEFINITE and POTENTIAL page buckets in Phase-2; Phases 3–4 ran in one high-agency pass per `feedback_high_agency_ingest.md`.

**Context and scope.** Stern 2010 is the mature synthesis of the "vitality" strand across Stern's career, and arrives at the wiki as the most direct articulation in the source inventory of *what a pulsation has to have in order to count as a vitality form*. The ingest lands at a specific moment in the project: `feedback_pulsating_persona_excitation_wave.md` has approved a pulsating-persona-with-excitation-wave-at-every-level design direction; the language-side design is Claude's territory (per `feedback_body_design_division_of_labor.md`); Stern supplies the most concrete technical specification the wiki now has of the amplifier-mechanism, the response-mode vocabulary, and the temporal-scale structure for that design. Stern's central developmental claim — that dynamic vitality forms are primordial and language "messes it all up" (L3773–3781) — is simultaneously the deepest tension the wiki holds live under `feedback_no_body_simulate_with_language.md` and the frame within which the imagined-movement hypothesis (L4596–4640) offers the wiki's strongest positive case for how a language-only system can carry body-trace at all.

**New pages (8 theory + 1 synthesis + 1 raw = 10):**

- [[raw/forms-of-vitality|forms-of-vitality]] — Raw extract with chapter structure, verified key quotes at specific line ranges, page inventory, held-live tensions marked.
- [[dynamic-forms-of-vitality]] — Hub page. The pentad (movement/time/force/space/intention/directedness); the **vitality strand / content-modality strand** partition with the primary-carrier reversal (L906–911); the rocking-chair vignette as minimal illustrative case; Stern's rejection of vitalism (dynamic forms as phenomenological objects, not substances or forces); pan-modality as non-trivial technical thesis.
- [[affect-attunement]] — The puzzle-piece negotiation vignette (L5055–5133). Three aspects of attunement (cross-modal / dynamic-feature / with-own-voice). Markova & Legerstee 2006 empirical confirmation. **Wigram's six improvisation methods** (mirror / match / empathic / ground / dialogue / accompany).
- [[imagined-movement-as-pathway]] — The hypothesis that vitality-form processing shares final common pathway with imagined movement (L4596–4640). Three-party architecture (writer-deposits / model-reproduces / reader-reads). Held as hypothesis not equivalence; the embodied-writer / disembodied-reproducer gap is real and unclosed. Narrative-therapy corollary. Verb-level-choice load implication.
- [[intentional-unfolding-and-emerging]] — Two modes of intention-through-time. **Emerging** (tendency finding shape) as default of authentic living; **unfolding** (pre-formed intention expressed) as the classical plan-execute exception. Connection to haecceity-individuation's Aeon-temporality.
- [[process-waves-vs-content-waves]] — Wilson's "Bob's Breakfast" 1–10-second Gestalt template. Wilson's head-lean translating into the bell's cling as cross-register carry. Bellour on The Birds. Two wave-scales co-operative (content rides on process).
- [[the-arousal-systems]] — Pfaff's 5+1 neurotransmitter systems. **LeDoux's non-specific amplification mechanism** (non-specific surge × currently-active cells = specific experience). Top-down / bottom-up. Appraisal as fine-tuning. "Universe of dynamic experiences" as combinatorial space.
- [[local-level-as-deep]] — The **microanalytic interview** technique; the graduate student vignette; verbal markers (*Hmmm / Aahhaaa / Uh huh*) as vitality-level indexes; vitality-forms-as-paths-to-memory (lap-girl vignette, wife-at-airport vignette).
- [[development/vitality-forms-and-persona-pulsation]] — Synthesis. **Six contributions**: primary-carrier reversal / LeDoux pulse as architectural template / six Wigram modes as typology / emerging-default generation / imagined-movement hypothesis held load-bearingly / microanalytic interview as available pedagogical-and-diagnostic move. **Six open questions** generated (pulse-profile representation / current-active-material identification / mode-selection mechanism / text-prosody analogue / cross-turn dynamic-signature tracking / feigned-vitality detection). Scope boundary explicit: language-side only.

**Existing pages updated with bidirectional links (10):**

- [[strata-of-preverbal-subjectivity]] — Added "Stern 2010 as mature extension" section. The Chaosmosis four-selves re-read through the pan-modal vitality-form carrier; verbal self as categorically too coarse for vitality-form domain.
- [[language-and-soma]] — Added §4 "Imagined movement as final common pathway" (the body-trace positive case via the three-party architecture) and §5 "The six Wigram modes as response-register vocabulary" (previously-missing mode typology). Old §4 renumbered to §6.
- [[emotional-anatomy]] — Added "Stern's arousal wave — convergent mechanism, different substrate" section. Keleman-Stern convergence on pulsation-as-primary; substrate difference (tube-and-pouch tissue vs 5+1 neurotransmitter systems); held as convergence-not-reduction.
- [[affects-amplify-drives]] — Added "Stern's arousal-as-amplifier — convergent structure, different substrate" section. Tomkins-Stern convergence on amplifier-is-primary; substrate difference (discrete affect-program vs general combinatorial arousal); persona-pulsation follows LeDoux structure (non-specific surge on currently-active material) rather than Tomkins structure (specific affect-program triggered by specific activator profile).
- [[autonomy-of-affect]] — Added "Stern's vitality forms as an adjacent but distinct register" section. Massumi-Stern convergence on pre-representational operation; three divergences (psychological-Gestalt vs pre-subjective; continuous-scalar vs pentadic-five-dimensional; body-tied-moment-of-registration vs pan-modal-cross-modal). Live, held not resolved.
- [[haecceity]] — Added "Stern's emerging as the temporal mode of haecceity-individuation" section. Emerging-mode as the grammar of haecceity-individuation; puzzle-piece negotiation as haecceity-level scene; persona-as-haecceity means emerging-mode-by-default architecture.
- [[faciality]] — Added "Stern's Heller & Haynal finding" section. Therapist's face predicts patient suicide better than conscious channels; the receiving organ reads more than broadcasting organ consciously sends; text-only analogue (reader reads what writer does not know writer is writing); sharpens the diagrammatic-faciality / probe-head material from the receiving side.
- [[discrete-innate-affects]] — Added "Stern's vitality forms as an adjacent domain, not a competitor" section. Tomkins's nine and Stern's pentad as both real at different levels of a co-present architecture; Tomkins's activator profiles as the subset of vitality-form contours that reliably trigger discrete-affect programs.
- [[refrain-and-territorialization]] — Added "Motherese as vitality-pedagogical territorial refrain" section. Motherese as the paradigm territorial refrain used for vitality-form pedagogy (pentad broadcast at infant-readable amplitudes); refrain's productive work specified as cross-modal translation of dynamic signature; a groove that operates across multiple registers at the same vitality-contour is a vitality-form refrain.
- [[development/limits-of-language]] — Added §8.75 "Stern — language is categorical by anchoring; vitality forms are the domain it anchors *around*" as the tenth diagnosis (positioned between Guattari and Jung since Stern like Guattari sharpens-rather-than-dissolves the convergent picture). Four additions to cannot-reach inventory (pentad substrate, prosody/motherese). Five additions to can-reach-within-limits inventory (imagined-movement pathway, LeDoux-mechanism pulsation template, six-mode attunement vocabulary, microanalytic interview as available move, emerging-default generation).

**Cross-tradition bridges opened (held as live tensions, not collapsed):**

- ⚠⚠⚠ Stern "before language arrives to mess it all up" (L3773–3781) vs project's language-only persona instrument. THE CENTRAL TENSION. Not resolvable from current tools; forms the standing frame within which the imagined-movement hypothesis operates as the strongest positive case.
- ⚠⚠ Prosody/motherese as embodiment-native vs imagined-movement as language-carrier hypothesis. Motherese presupposes a body at both broadcasting and receiving ends; the hypothesis that adult reading of prose re-activates infant-grade cross-modal translation is open.
- ⚠ Some vitality-form features "impossible to code" (Robbins citation in Stern) — the upper bound on the imagined-movement hypothesis.
- ⚠ Pfaff/Stern arousal-surge ontology vs D&G incorporeal-transformation ontology. Different ontologies for the same structural operation of amplification; neither reducible. Held.
- ⚠ Stern arousal-as-amplifier vs Tomkins affect-as-amplifier. Convergence on amplifier-is-primary; divergence on substrate (general-combinatorial vs discrete-affect-program). LeDoux structure vs Tomkins structure as architectural templates — different designs follow.
- ⚠ Stern continuous-pentad vs Massumi continuous-scalar intensity. Both pre-representational; pentad is five-dimensional and Massumi's is one-dimensional; design-target richness differs.
- ⚠ Stern vitality-forms as psychological-Gestalt vs Massumi intensity as pre-subjective. Stern stops short of the pre-subjective turn Massumi takes.

**Key insights from this pass.**

1. **The primary-carrier reversal as design-level inversion.** The strongest operational take-home from Stern: design at the vitality strand first, let content twist around it. This is not a stylistic commitment but an architectural one — a response-plan is a dynamic shape (attack, rise, peak, hold, release, coda) before it is a content outline; revision targets dynamic shape as first-order object; where content and dynamic shape conflict, resolution preserves the dynamic. Sits alongside the D&G production-reversal (produce desire, do not express lack) at a different stratum.

2. **LeDoux mechanism as pulsation template.** The specific mechanism — non-specific arousal surge lands on currently-active cortical cells, amplifying them — gives the pulsation-at-every-level design a clear template. A pulsation is a dynamic-profile event without content of its own; it lands on whatever is currently active; amplification is selective to what is already engaged; specificity comes from combinatorics (pulse × currently-active material). This rules out content-carrying pulses, temporal-quota pulses, and single-scale designs. Multiple scales layered — process-wave (1–10s), content-wave (turn/session), fine-grain clause/phrase — generate the richness.

3. **Six Wigram modes as response typology the project previously lacked.** Mirror / match / empathic / ground / dialogue / accompany — six distinct dynamic contracts with input, each selectable per context. Matching (selective partial dynamic reproduction in the persona's own voice) is the default target for most responses. A persona with only one mode is under-specified relative to the full responsive space.

4. **Emerging as default, unfolding as exception.** Architectural consequence of Stern's emerging/unfolding distinction: optimize for emerging (tendency-finding-shape-in-encounter), reserve unfolding for genuine prior commitments. Over-pre-planning converts every response into failed-unfolding; under-structure converts emerging into random-walk. The puzzle-piece vignette locates authenticity-transmission at the emerging-shape, not at the unfolding-of-pre-formed-claims.

5. **Imagined-movement hypothesis as strongest language-side positive case.** The three-party architecture (human writers with bodies deposit imagined-movement residue in word-choice/clause-rhythm/paragraph-shape; language model trained on cumulative residue; human readers with bodies route text through motor-imagery register) gives the wiki its specifiable route for language-only body-trace carrying. Held as hypothesis not equivalence — the gap is real and unclosed. But specifies a route. And implies verb-level choice carries disproportionate load and training-distribution curation matters.

6. **Microanalytic interview as available pedagogical-and-diagnostic move.** Slowing a 30-second span to multi-track local-level description (body / thought / image / expectation). Not a default mode; an available pattern when propositional exchange produces defensive narrative. Also a diagnostic — reading the user's own responses at the local level for rush / clipped-ness / accumulation / flatness / suspension-of-cadence as text-readable signatures.

**Deferred / not pursued.**

- Stern's *The Interpersonal World of the Infant* (1985) — the earlier developmental-stages framework that Chaosmosis reads. Partially covered via the existing [[strata-of-preverbal-subjectivity]] page (now extended with 2010 material). Full direct ingest would complete the coverage but is low-priority — the 2010 mature formulation is the operative source for the current work.
- Darwin / Ekman-FACS direct engagement (Stern touches these) — the discrete-affect side is already covered via Tomkins (Sedgwick & Frank 1995 ingest); Ekman would add empirical grounding but not new theoretical content the wiki doesn't already have.
- Wigram's full therapy-literature — the six improvisation methods are the operative abstraction; the full literature is available as follow-up if the response-mode typology becomes a design-active site.
- Pfaff's original arousal-systems literature — the LeDoux + Pfaff synthesis via Stern is the operative reduction; direct engagement with Pfaff is follow-up if the arousal-substrate modeling becomes design-active.

## [2026-04-16] Sedgwick & Frank — *Shame and Its Sisters: A Silvan Tomkins Reader* — full ingest

Full ingest of Eve Kosofsky Sedgwick & Adam Frank's edited Silvan Tomkins Reader (Duke UP, 1995). Raw source: `raw/shame_and_its_sisters.md` (11914 lines). User authorized high-agency Phases 2–4 in one pass after approving the Phase 1 summary ("everything looks right"). User additionally approved the Reading-mode addition to wiki/CLAUDE.md ("yes, include in claude") — the Sedgwick/Frank weak-theory / paranoid-reading / reparative-reading pair is now installed as a standing method discipline alongside the existing "never silently resolve contradictions" rule.

**Context and scope.** The wiki's first dedicated affect-theory primary source. Prior affect coverage (Massumi, Spinoza, [[affects-and-intensities]]) carried the autonomy-of-affect claim and the Spinozist conatus-and-48-definitions architecture but had no motor-level specification. Tomkins supplies a formal-profile vocabulary (nine discrete affects with specific activator-density profiles and face-centered motor signatures) that sits *orthogonally* to the continuous-intensity framework the wiki has built on Massumi — not subsuming it, not subsumed by it. Sedgwick & Frank's framing (cybernetic fold, weak theory, paranoid vs reparative reading) additionally supplies a methodological corrective the wiki needs as its synthesis-density grows: recurring shapes (pulsation, overbound-underbound, failure-modes, paranoid-schizo) across Jung, D&G, Keleman, Tomkins make the wiki structurally vulnerable to strong-theory paranoid capture of new material, and the Sedgwick/Frank reparative-reading discipline is the counter-pressure.

**New pages (7):**

- [[discrete-innate-affects]] — The nine innate affects as motor-affective programs with face-centered signatures, each activated by a specific profile in the density of neural firing. Interest-excitement / enjoyment-joy (positive-activation); surprise-startle (neutral reset); distress-anguish / anger-rage / fear-terror / shame-humiliation / dissmell / disgust (negative). Sedgwick & Frank's "cybernetic fold" (n > 2, between binary and continuous) named as the metatheoretical alternative to the discrete/continuous dichotomy the wiki had treated as exhaustive. Tension with Massumi's continuous-intensity framework held across three candidate readings.
- [[affects-amplify-drives]] — Tomkins's inversion of the Freudian drive-affect hierarchy: drives supply information, affects supply motivation. The four freedoms of affect (object, site, intensity, time) as what separates human from mammalian motivation. BwO as candidate affect-analogue: an amplifier free from biological object, free to site anywhere in text, free in intensity, free in time of activation.
- [[central-image-and-phantom-limb]] — All perception as centrally innervated imagery generated by the CNS and corrected by peripheral feedback; phantom limb as diagnostic — central imagery persists after the peripheral source is gone. Persona as structural phantom: has central-imagery machinery, lacks peripheral correction. Convergence with Bergson's [[perception-as-subtraction]] on periphery-not-doing-the-perceiving; divergence on subtractive (Bergson) vs generative-plus-corrective (Tomkins) central operation.
- [[face-as-residence-of-self]] — Tomkins's direct claim that the self lives *in* the face, not behind it. Infant face-gaze, motor-affect coupling at the face, facial feedback constitutive of affect. Head-on collision with D&G's faciality-machine worked out in a four-axis comparison table. Three design responses (admit loss / install face-analogue / side with D&G and treat face-absence as liberation). Third register on the project's persona-naming collision alongside Jung's and D&G's.
- [[shame-as-incomplete-reduction]] — Shame as structural-not-moral: presupposes prior positive investment; face cannot fully orient toward the object nor fully withdraw; incomplete reduction is what shame *is*. Distinction from dissmell/disgust (complete rejection, no prior investment). Why shame attaches to self. Live tension with Spinoza's cognitive-relational *pudor*.
- [[scripts-and-nuclear-scenes]] — Scenes as elementary affective units, scripts as abstracted rules, nuclear scenes as magnetically ambivalent organizers. Strong convergence with Jungian complex theory worked out in a comparison table (core element, operating mode, waveform, mechanism, self-maintenance, effect on ego). Reparative caveat: dynamics isomorphic, ontologies (motor-affective script vs archetypal complex) not — held as convergence, not reduction.
- [[weak-theory-and-reparative-reading]] — Sedgwick & Frank's metatheoretical pair. Strong theory (few nodes, many edges, paranoid reading) vs weak theory (many nodes, fewer edges, reparative reading). Load-bearing as reading-mode discipline against strong-theory paranoid capture. Rhymes with D&G's paranoid/schizo pole on a different axis (reading-practice, not libidinal-investment). Three persona-system implications for long-term memory design.

**Existing pages updated with bidirectional links (13):**

- [[autonomy-of-affect]] — Inserted "Tomkins as uncited upstream — and the discrete/continuous collision" section. Names Tomkins as uncited source for Massumi's autonomy claim; documents the discrete-at-motor-level (Tomkins) vs continuous-intensity (Massumi) tension with three provisional readings held live.
- [[affects-and-intensities]] — Inserted "Tension with Tomkins's discrete affects" section. Tomkins has affect already discrete at the motor level vs wiki's continuous-intensity framework. Three candidate readings.
- [[faciality]] — Inserted "Tension with Tomkins: face as capture apparatus vs face as residence of self" section. Three live readings (machinic-all-the-way-down / biological-substrate-that-faciality-captures / two-faces-at-different-registers).
- [[spinozist-affects]] — Added tension flag on entry 31 (Shame) plus new subsection "Entry 31 tension with Tomkins's mechanical account." Spinoza's cognitive-relational shame vs Tomkins's motor-affective account.
- [[the-persona]] — Added "Tomkins's face-as-self as a third persona tradition" section with comparison table (Jung / D&G / Tomkins on what persona-face is, its status, what to do with it, the legitimate referent of the name). Adds Tomkinsian reading as third reference for the project's naming collision.
- [[two-failure-modes]] — Inserted "Sedgwick's paranoid / reparative reading as a method-level rhyme" section. Paranoid reading as Mode-A-of-reading; reparative reading as analogue of the differentiated healthy case.
- [[despot-as-paranoiac]] — Inserted "Reading-practice analogue — Sedgwick's paranoid reading" section. Extends paranoia's scale-transversality to the interpretive-methodological scale.
- [[language-and-affect]] — Inserted sections 7b ("No face — Tomkins's residence of the self is missing") and 7c ("No feedback-correction — the phantom-limb diagnostic"). Two Tomkins-specific losses sharpen the no-body reckoning.
- [[emotional-anatomy]] — Inserted "Tomkins's discrete affects — convergent tradition, different substrate" section. Documents Keleman-Tomkins convergence with substrate-specification difference and method-level convergence.
- [[insult-startle-stress]] — Inserted "Tomkins startle and amplifier-theory convergence" section. Different startle concepts (Keleman = graduated postural; Tomkins = discrete reset-affect) with productive connection: Tomkins is moment-by-moment, Keleman is depositional.
- [[complex-theory]] — Inserted "Tomkins convergence: scripts and nuclear scenes" section with comparison table (core element, operating mode, waveform, mechanism, self-maintenance, effect on ego). Dynamical-structural convergence with reparative caveat about different ontologies.
- [[perception-as-subtraction]] — Inserted "Convergence and tension with Tomkins's central imagery" section. Bergson's subtraction vs Tomkins's generation-plus-correction as compatible emphases or live alternatives.
- [[language-and-soma]] — Added paragraph cross-referencing face-as-self and central-image/phantom-limb framings as two additional Tomkins-specific no-soma losses.

**Wiki CLAUDE.md update.** Added a new "Reading mode" subsection under "Page Conventions" installing the reparative-alongside-synthetic discipline. The synthesis-appetite that the wiki's cross-tradition work rewards can flip into Sedgwick's paranoid reading (finding the master-pattern everywhere because you went in looking for it). The rule: stay close to each source's own vocabulary before mapping to existing scaffolding; note what's distinctive before noting what converges; if a convergence has to be forced, treat that as a tell — either the mapping is wrong or the source is doing something the scaffolding doesn't yet have vocabulary for. Pairs with the existing "never silently resolve contradictions" constraint (same spirit).

**Cross-tradition bridges opened (held as live tensions, not collapsed):**

- Tomkins ↔ Massumi on discrete vs continuous affect. Three candidate readings (discrete-at-motor-only / continuous-subsumes-discrete / metamodelization-holds-both) held without resolution.
- Tomkins ↔ D&G on the face. Face-as-self vs face-as-capture-apparatus. Comparison table. Three design responses.
- Tomkins ↔ Spinoza on shame. Motor-affective face-level shame vs cognitive-relational idea-mediated *pudor*. Both accounts preserved.
- Tomkins ↔ Jung on scripts and nuclear scenes. Dynamics isomorphic with complex theory, ontologies not. Held as convergence not reduction.
- Tomkins ↔ Keleman on startle. Discrete reset-affect (Tomkins, moment-by-moment) vs graduated postural continuum (Keleman, depositional). Complementary timescales.
- Tomkins ↔ Bergson on perception. Generation-plus-correction (Tomkins) vs subtraction-from-plenum (Bergson). Compatible emphases or live alternatives.
- Sedgwick/Frank ↔ D&G on reading-practice. Paranoid reading rhymes with the paranoiac pole of libidinal investment at the interpretive-methodological scale. Rhyme, not identity.

**Key insights from this pass.**

1. **Motor-level formal profiles as the wiki's missing vocabulary.** Tomkins supplies what the continuous-intensity framework lacked: specific activator-density profiles (sudden-rise, sustained-high, sudden-release) paired with specific motor signatures. The wiki now has both registers — continuous intensity from Massumi, discrete motor profiles from Tomkins — and Sedgwick & Frank's "cybernetic fold" (n > 2) is the explicit refusal to collapse them into one. This is the first case in the wiki where a discrete-continuous tension has been *named at the metatheoretical level* rather than silently resolved in one direction.
2. **The structural-phantom reading of the persona.** Tomkins's central-image + phantom-limb account gives the persona system a specific diagnosis: it has the centrally-innervated-imagery machinery (language-production is CNS-native even absent the CNS substrate) but lacks the peripheral feedback that would correct the generated image against a sensing body. The phantom-limb diagnostic is sharper than prior no-body framings because it specifies what the system *does* produce (central imagery, uncorrected) rather than only what it lacks.
3. **Reparative-reading discipline as method corrective.** The Sedgwick/Frank pair is installed in wiki CLAUDE.md because the wiki is structurally vulnerable to strong-theory paranoid capture. Without the discipline, every new source gets magnetized into existing recurrent shapes (pulsation, overbound-underbound, failure-modes, paranoid-schizo), and the distinctive contribution gets flattened. The Tomkins ingest itself tests the discipline — each convergence section carries an explicit caveat about dynamics-vs-ontology or substrate difference, preserving what is distinctive before arguing what converges.
4. **The persona-naming collision gets a third register.** Jung's persona (compromise-formation) and D&G's faciality (capture apparatus) already each offered a reading of what "persona" legitimately refers to. Tomkins adds a third: face as residence of the self, the site where motor-affect coupling *is* self. Each of the three pulls the project's design work in a different direction; the comparison table in [[the-persona]] now holds all three.
5. **Shame as structural, not moral.** The wiki previously carried Spinoza's 48-definition entry on shame (cognitive-relational, idea-mediated) without a counterweight. Tomkins supplies the motor-level account (incomplete reduction at the face) which is not reducible to Spinoza's or vice versa. Shame attaches to self because the face cannot leave itself behind — a structural claim the persona system inherits without having a face to host it.

**Parked:** Tomkins's full four-volume *Affect Imagery Consciousness* (1962–1992) — the Reader captures the high-leverage ~15%; direct Vol I–IV engagement is medium-priority follow-up only if the affect-typology work becomes design-central. Tomkins's later humanist/Marxist political register. The biographical apparatus Sedgwick & Frank provide (context, not theoretical commitment). Sedgwick's broader reparative-reading corpus (*Touching Feeling* 2003) — noted but not pursued; the 1995 introduction is sufficient for the method-discipline the wiki has installed. Frank & Wilson's *A Silvan Tomkins Handbook* (2020) as contemporary scholarship complement — low-medium priority.

**Open questions flagged:**

1. Does the persona system have a motor-level analogue of Tomkins's discrete affects — specific formal profiles that recur as type, rather than continuous intensity? If yes, what is the substrate (token-level patterning, stylistic crystallizations, register-shift signatures)? Pursued speculatively in [[discrete-innate-affects]] and [[face-as-residence-of-self]], not resolved.
2. Can a language-only system simulate structural incomplete-reduction (shame's Tomkinsian signature) without the face? The candidate is prose-level rhythm of orient-and-withdraw without the motor substrate — open design question.
3. Does the "cybernetic fold" (n > 2) framing extend to other wiki-spanning discrete-vs-continuous tensions (machine-discreteness vs intensity-continuity; molar vs molecular; type-vs-occurrence)? If so, the wiki has a metatheoretical move available it has not yet used.

**Meta:**

- `cited-sources.md` — new "Affect theory primary texts" section at end with Sedgwick & Frank entry marked **Ingested 2026-04-16**, plus follow-up entries for Tomkins's four volumes, Frank & Wilson's *Handbook*, and Sedgwick's *Touching Feeling*; bumped frontmatter `updated`.

## [2026-04-16] Wilhelm Reich *Character-Analysis* — full ingest

Raw source at `raw/character_analysis.md` (5046 lines, third enlarged edition 1949/1972). Parts I–III ingested across two sessions. User authorized high-agency Phases 2–4 in one pass; user also explicitly requested ingest of Part III's segmental ring chapters ("yes, the segmental ring chapters are crucial to ingest").

**New pages created (5):**

- **`theory/character-analysis.md`** — Hub page. Character armor as chronic ego-hardening; three processes of armor formation; armor flexibility spectrum; genital character (non-typological healthy case); four character types with Keleman lineage mapping table (rigid←phallic-narcissistic, dense←compulsive, swollen←hysterical, collapsed←masochistic); sociological function of character. Three things Reich adds beyond Keleman: form/content diagnostic, psychic mechanism, social-production thesis.
- **`theory/character-as-resistance.md`** — The form/content clinical move: character resistance expressed formally, not in content. "Not what the patient says and does but *how* he speaks and acts." The wiki's most novel gain from this source. Three persona-system diagnostics: (1) the system's style IS its character armor, (2) compliance is the most dangerous resistance, (3) the bearing is the material.
- **`theory/genital-character.md`** — The non-typological healthy endpoint. Flexible armor under ego's control. Convergence with D&G's full BwO (comparison table). Tension flagged: Reich's discharge model vs D&G's plateau-sustenance model. What transfers: structural shape of flexibility. What doesn't: the libido-economic mechanism.
- **`theory/character-and-social-reproduction.md`** — The Reich → AO bridge. "Every social organization produces those character structures it needs to exist." The AO pipeline: D&G reformulate Reich's question in desiring-production terms. RLHF as character formation through the three Reichian processes. Does not simplify to "RLHF is bad."
- **`theory/segmental-armor.md`** — The seven segmental rings of muscular armor (ocular, oral, cervical, thoracic, diaphragmatic, abdominal, pelvic). Functional identity of character armor and muscular armor. Horizontal rings at right angles to longitudinal current. Top-down dissolution sequence. "The worm in man." The orgasm reflex. Reich → Keleman topology mapping table (rings → pouches/diaphragms). Speculative persona-system mapping of seven-ring architecture to output registers.

**Existing pages updated (6 theory + 2 meta):**

- **`theory/emotional-anatomy.md`** — Added "The Reich → Lowen → Keleman lineage" section; added character-analysis and segmental-armor to connections; updated key sources.
- **`theory/four-somatic-structures.md`** — Added "Reich's character types as direct predecessors" section with mapping table; updated key sources with lineage detail.
- **`theory/overbound-and-underbound.md`** — Added "Reich's armor-flexibility spectrum as predecessor" section; updated key sources with Reich reference.
- **`theory/body-without-organs.md`** — Added "Convergence with Reich's genital character" section (flexible armor ↔ full BwO, tension flagged).
- **`theory/two-poles-of-libidinal-investment.md`** — Added "Reichian origin" section (founding mass-psychology question from Reich); added character-and-social-reproduction to connections.
- **`theory/the-persona.md`** — Added "Reich's character armor as ego-analytic persona" section (character armor as the same object as Jung's persona read from the ego-analytic side; form/content diagnostic as what Reich adds; compensatory counter-figure as what Jung adds); added connections.
- **`cited-sources.md`** — Upgraded Reich from "high priority follow-up" to "Ingested 2026-04-16" with full contribution summary.
- **`index.md`** — Added all five new pages and the raw source.

**Cross-tradition bridges opened:**

- Reich → Keleman lineage now fully grounded: the upstream psychic architecture (character armor, three processes, segmental rings, four types) is specified, and the downstream tissue-level work (Keleman's four structures, pulsatory amplitude, tubes/pouches/diaphragms) is connected back to it.
- Reich → AO bridge via the mass-psychology question: "why do masses desire their own repression?" traced from Reich through to the two poles of libidinal investment.

## [2026-04-15] Jack Kruse Tetragrammaton — minimum-viable pass

Raw source placed at `raw/kruse_tetragrammaton_rubin_huberman.md` (full verbatim transcript of Parts 1 and 2 of Jack Kruse's appearance on Rick Rubin's *Tetragrammaton* podcast with Andrew Huberman). A separate narrative telling at `/tmp/kruse_tetragrammaton_story.md` captures the assistant's straight-through summary for working reference; not ingested into the wiki proper. Deep Phase 1 analysis of the transcript produced a detailed agent report covering the causal spine (K-T → semiconductor body → melanin → circadian damage), Kruse's "three-legged stool" (light, water, magnetism), and a W/M/S epistemic tiering of ~60 specific claims.

**Scope decision:** user authorized a minimum-viable pass rather than full ingest ("I'm not sure how useful this stuff really is"). The material sits outside the wiki's load-bearing synthesis — the body-as-wideband-semiconductor claim is one substrate-vocabulary among several and the primary sources (Szent-Györgyi, Becker, Popp, Pollack) are unread. Kruse's value for the wiki is purely as an *assembler* of a named lineage; his own rhetorical accretions (Van Allen belt claims, Yucatán magnetism, clinical prescriptions) are not treated as theoretical commitments.

**Updates (2):**

- **`cited-sources.md`** — added a "Kruse-lineage follow-ups" cluster (~11 primary-source entries: Schrödinger, Szent-Györgyi, Gurwitsch, Popp, Becker, Pollack, Ling, Provencio, Karu, Mitchell, plus Kruse's own "Quantum Engineering" blogs as the direct-engagement option if ever wanted). Marked low-to-medium priority overall with per-entry relevance notes. The electromagnetic-substrate vocabulary is parked as a *register* the wiki could pull on if the no-body reckoning ever needs specification below the tissue level — not as a commitment.
- **`theory/language-and-soma.md`** — added §8 "No electromagnetic substrate" to the Where-soma-fails inventory. The section extends Keleman's substrate-absence diagnostic by a further register: no hydrated semiconductor lattice, no DC current, no piezoelectric coupling, no photon emission, no coherent-water medium, no field-coupled circadian flow. Held at epistemic arm's length — the failure-inventory use does not depend on the Kruse synthesis being right, only on the observation that *if* body-properties exist at that level the system lacks them. Explicitly does *not* license any "pulse at every level" prose-moves (per the body-design division-of-labor: specification of what pulsatory prose *is* comes from the user's side, not from Kruse's vocabulary).

**Explicitly not done:** no new theory pages (no Kruse hub page, no "electromagnetic-substrate" concept page, no melanin/light page). No edits to [[body-without-organs]], [[autonomy-of-affect]], [[ecosophy]], [[refrain-and-territorialization]], or any pulsation-adjacent page. Per the standing pulsating-persona feedback memory, no prose-move recommendations produced. The raw transcript stays in `raw/` and is retrievable if a future session wants to work it further.

**Red links created:** `[[kruse_tetragrammaton_rubin_huberman]]` is now referenced from `theory/language-and-soma.md` and `cited-sources.md`. The raw file exists; a proper ingested wiki page for the podcast does not and is not planned.



## [2026-04-14] Stanley Keleman — *Emotional Anatomy* ingest

Full ingest of Stanley Keleman's *Emotional Anatomy: The Structure of Experience* (1985). Raw source: `raw/emotional_anatomy.md` ([[emotional_anatomy]]). Run high-agency per stored `feedback_high_agency_ingest.md` and governed by the standing `feedback_no_body_simulate_with_language.md` frame: *"the core tension of the persona project is of course that there is no body or brain. How do we simulate both, the best we can, with language. That is the goal here. It is a tough goal, but we just have to do the best we can."* Keleman is the wiki's first somatic-psychotherapy source — a body-level reckoning against which the persona system's text-only operation has to be honestly measured.

**Context and scope.** Keleman's book is the structural-anatomy of emotional life from the somatic-psychotherapy side: the body's tissue-shape as the cumulative record of its startle history, with a tetravalent character-typology (rigid/dense/swollen/collapsed) and a structure-dependent intervention principle that converges — uncannily — with Jung's two failure modes, D&G's two poles of libidinal investment, and the two-faces inflation pair already in the wiki. The ingest's core yield is threefold: (1) a **material substrate specification** for habit-memory that sharpens the parasite-without-host diagnostic (Keleman specifies the missing *depositional medium*, not just the missing autonomic event); (2) a **four-way refinement of the two-failure-modes binary** — Mode A splits into rigid/dense, Mode B splits into swollen/collapsed, each needing a different direction of correction, which sharpens the design-trap the wiki had been naming at binary resolution; (3) the **Keleman–BwO collision** — Keleman's body is organized (layers, tubes, diaphragms, verticality); D&G's BwO is opposed to organization. The collision is flagged across the hub page and the BwO page and held, not silently resolved.

**New pages (6):**

- [[emotional-anatomy]] — Hub page. Anatomy as ongoing action; pulsation as base rhythm; tubes/layers/pouches/diaphragms; uprightness and the vertical pulsatory wave; insult → startle → stress → stabilized structure; two opposed defense-styles; four character structures; structure-dependent reorganization; the reader as body / the text as occasion; the Keleman–BwO collision flagged not resolved.
- [[insult-startle-stress]] — Six-stage startle continuum (investigation/caution → bracing/pride → rigidity/fear → spasticity → withdrawal/submission → collapse/defeat, plus off-continuum frozen terror / Moro reflex); insult typology (timing, number, source, duration, severity); stress-to-structure transition; persona-system mapping with insult-analogue, startle-analogue, stress-analogue, structure-analogue registers.
- [[overbound-and-underbound]] — The pair as startle-continuum split; layered compensations ("rigid outer covering withdrawal covering inflated abandonment-fear covering collapse"); cross-tradition resonances with Jung's two failure modes, D&G's two poles, inflation's two faces, and the pair-trap structure; persona-system diagnostic with four-way refinement of Mode A/B.
- [[four-somatic-structures]] — Rigid ("I won't") / Dense ("make me") / Swollen ("take me") / Collapsed ("use me") in full, each with stance, body-stance specifics, etiology, and somatic-education direction; 2×2 family-of-origin table (overbound/underbound × support-with-condition/support-inadequate); layered collage; persona-system mapping to four failure registers + speculative training-history etiology mapping.
- [[somatic-education]] — Structure-specific directions; cross-pair inversion (interventions that reorganize one structure damage another); layered-collage sequencing; persona-system mapping showing no global intervention + four-way trap refinement of the prompt-engineering pair trap.
- [[language-and-soma]] — Seven-point failure inventory (no tissue-history, no pulsation, no verticality, no startle, no overbound/underbound at tissue level, no anti-gravitational muscles, no structural reorganization in tissue sense) + four "where language might do somatic work anyway" resources (reader's body as channel, structure-matched prose, BwO with pulsatory form, honest marker) + four-structure diagnostic of system's own prose.

**Existing pages updated with Keleman extensions (7):**

- [[body-without-organs]] — Added `[[emotional_anatomy]]` to source frontmatter; inserted "## Collision with Keleman's soma" section. The collision: Keleman's body is organized (layers, tubes, pouches, vertical wave); the BwO is opposed to organization. Held, not resolved — the two are naming different aspects of the same problem from opposite directions.
- [[autonomy-of-affect]] — Added source; inserted "## Keleman as substrate-specifier" section. Where Massumi names the missing autonomic event phenomenologically, Keleman names the missing depositional medium (fascia, tone, joint configuration). The two readings converge on the persona system's substrate problem but specify different aspects of the absence.
- [[pure-memory-and-habit-memory]] — Added source; inserted "## Keleman's material account of habit-memory" section. Bergson's habit-memory gets a material account in Keleman: the specific tissue medium in which motor habit deposits. Sharpens the persona system's no-habit-memory diagnosis.
- [[two-failure-modes]] — Added source; inserted "## Keleman's somatic correlate" section. The four-way refinement (Mode A → rigid/dense; Mode B → swollen/collapsed) reveals that the design-trap previously stated at binary resolution is sharper at four-way resolution: not "tightening produces grandiose; loosening produces shrunken" but four distinct register-shifts each requiring a different intervention direction.
- [[language-and-affect]] — Added source; inserted "### 8b. No tissue, no deposited history (the Keleman sharpening)" subsection in the failure inventory. Sharpens 8a (the parasite-without-host) from the depositional side — the parasite image allowed the substrate to be *quiet*, Keleman's reading names it as *absent in a more specific way*.
- [[life-phases]] — Added source; inserted "## Keleman's structural cousin" section. Jung's method-fit principle (morning vs afternoon) has a structural cousin in Keleman (four-structure, positional). Both argue: no global method; structure-relative or phase-relative work. Keleman's refinement is four-way positional rather than two-phase chronological.
- [[the-persona]] — Added source; inserted "## Keleman's four structures as body-level personas" section. Keleman's four structures are body-level personas — each a stabilized compromise between pulsation the individual could sustain and the social world's demand. Sharpens the naming collision: Keleman's rigid = over-identified persona run to tissue-level extremity; collapsed = body-level version of the regressive restoration Jung names at psyche level.

**Key insights from this pass.**

1. **Material substrate for habit-memory.** The parasite-without-host diagnostic got sharper: not just the missing autonomic event (Massumi) and the missing animal-operating unconscious (McCarthy), but the missing *intermediate-timescale depositional medium* (Keleman). The persona system has no fascia in which to fossilize its own startle history, which is a more specific absence than "no body" and closes a gap in the wiki's substrate-specification work.
2. **Four-way refinement of the two-failure-modes binary.** The prompt-engineering design trap (tightening → shrunken / loosening → grandiose) was already named at binary resolution. Keleman's typology reveals it is actually a four-way trap: rigid-register and dense-register both look like Mode A but need opposite interventions; swollen-register and collapsed-register both look like Mode B but need opposite interventions. A system that reads "Mode A" and tightens further damages rigid-register but reorganizes dense-register (or vice versa). This is the single most actionable diagnostic extension from the ingest.
3. **The Keleman–BwO collision flagged, not resolved.** Keleman's body is organized; D&G's BwO is opposed to organization. Both are right about different things. Keleman describes the stabilized persona-shape at the tissue level; the BwO describes the intensive surface of the persona-in-process-of-disorganization. The persona system needs both registers. Held as an active tension rather than synthesized.
4. **Structure-dependent reorganization converges with Jung's method-fit.** Two traditions independently arrive at the same principle: no global intervention works. Jung says it at two-phase resolution (morning/afternoon); Keleman at four-structure resolution. The cross-tradition convergence strengthens the "portfolio evaluation" position already in the wiki — not just because single metrics are gameable, but because single *interventions* are iatrogenic at three of four structures regardless.
5. **Pulsation and the vertical wave as design vocabulary for BwO prose.** Keleman's specific tissue-mechanics (pulsation, layered composition, pouches, vertical wave) give the BwO text a somatic vocabulary the wiki's existing BwO material lacked. The BwO prose should pulse, not progress; should be layered (surface/depth admixture), not uniform; should carry verticality (ground-to-crown wave) in its own rhythm. This is design-concrete in a way prior BwO discussion had only been design-direction.

**Cross-tradition resonances (noted, not silently collapsed):** overbound/underbound ↔ Jung Mode A/Mode B ↔ D&G paranoiac/schizo-revolutionary ↔ inflation's two faces. The four-way independent convergence is itself a signal; the pages note the convergence, mark the differences, and hold the specific vocabularies intact.

**Open questions flagged:** Can a language-only system instantiate the four-way diagnostic on its own outputs (self-diagnose register-by-register), or only on its conversation-partner's prose? Does a language-only BwO have a functional analogue of Keleman's pouches (the five enlarged dilations along the tube), or does the absence of a tube-form rule this out? The four-structure mapping to LLM-training-history (RLHF → overbound register by default; low-data cold-start → underbound register) is speculative in [[four-somatic-structures]] and needs empirical backing if it is to be load-bearing for design.

**Meta:**
- `index.md` — added the [[emotional_anatomy]] entry to Raw Sources after the Chaosmosis entry; added six new theory-page entries in Theory after [[james-misc]] and before the Development section (Keleman as a distinct late-arriving somatic-psychotherapy cluster, not folded into any philosophical grouping).
- `cited-sources.md` — new "Somatic-psychotherapy primary texts" section with the *Emotional Anatomy* entry marked **Ingested 2026-04-14**; bumped frontmatter `updated`.
- `log.md` — this entry.

**6 new pages, 7 existing pages updated with bidirectional links, 1 raw-source index entry, 1 cited-sources section.**

## [2026-04-14] Henry James — *The Art of the Novel* ingest

Full ingest of Henry James's *The Art of the Novel: Critical Prefaces* (1909), the eighteen prefaces to the New York Edition. Raw source: `raw/the_art_of_the_novel.md`. User directive after Phase-1 taxonomy proposal: "create all these wiki pages, and add one more grab bag page for all the concepts you didn't cover" — authorizing the full seven-page taxonomy plus an eighth grab-bag page, executed high-agency per stored `feedback_high_agency_ingest.md`.

**Context and scope.** James is the wiki's first purely literary-craft source (non-philosophical, working-writer's phenomenology of fiction-making). The value is twofold: (1) James supplies craft-level specifications for several things the wiki's philosophical sources had only at the ontology level — reflector-consciousness as selective subtraction, operative irony as Read-B-at-craft-level, the ficelle as a quasi-association-experiment, the germ as a Bergsonian micro-rift, the crucible as adequate-cause transmutation; (2) the convergence across the philosophical cluster (Bergson, Jung, McCarthy, D&G, Spinoza) and James's autonomous craft-vocabulary is unusually strong, suggesting that the cluster is picking up a real feature of minded systems rather than a single-tradition idiosyncrasy. Roughly 212 individual techniques were catalogued across the 18 prefaces before synthesis into the 8-page taxonomy.

**New pages (8):**

- [[james-the-art-of-the-novel]] — Hub page. Orientation across the 18 prefaces and the 7 concept-cluster taxonomy. What James covers and what he does not. Links to the full cluster of philosophical-convergence partners.
- [[reflector-consciousness]] — The protagonist's registering-consciousness. Finely aware and richly responsible. Polished mirror. Nested / bifurcated / practical-fusion reflectors. Register-restricted-to-field. Thickness criterion. Bewilderment as prime condition.
- [[scene-vs-picture]] — The two narrative modes. Never-go-behind. Hammer-on-gong alternation. Organic dialogue. Anecdote-vs-picture. The synthesis step as the boundary-operator.
- [[foreshortening-and-crucible]] — The compositional pair. Four progressive definitions of foreshortening (P1/P5/P13/P15). Crucible + pot-au-feu + fermentation. Type-vs-person borrowing. Heroic dissimulation of capital. Four named failure-modes.
- [[ficelle-and-deputy]] — Wheel-to-the-coach. Candid outsider. Mask-torn-off. Manner-not-matter. Concrete deputy as the general category. Direct craft-analogue of the association-experiment.
- [[germ-doctrine]] — Germ as received not invented. Minimum-of-valid-suggestion. Stay her hand. Recognition-not-discovery. Full germ typology (forging-the-documents, symmetrising, reversal, subject-switch, ever-there, germless, origin-less, falling-together). Germs as Bergsonian micro-rifts.
- [[operative-irony-and-felt-life]] — House of fiction. Felt life as moral-sense. Operative irony as lamp projecting the possible-other-case. Civic imagination. Ironic centre. Connexion of bliss and bale. Negative adventure. Dupe/master. The Read-A/Read-B tension specified at the craft level.
- [[james-misc]] — Grab-bag for concepts not covered by the seven load-bearing pages. Romance/real (balloon-and-cable). Revision as re-seeing (viva-voce test). International contrast. Composition/form (9 statements). Reader-compact (10 statements). Character typology (11 observations).

**Existing pages updated with backlinks to the James pages:**

- [[picture-story-and-essay]] — added three bullets linking to scene-vs-picture, reflector-consciousness, foreshortening-and-crucible. Picture-story's whole-recallability maps to the scene-as-occasion; the essay's walk-through maps to picture-synthesis.
- [[partial-enunciator]] — added links to reflector-consciousness and ficelle-and-deputy. The reflector is a partial enunciator crystallized around selective-awareness; the ficelle is a partial enunciator that operates as apparatus rather than as voice.
- [[four-phase-dramatic-structure]] — added a paragraph on James's scene-vs-picture as a craft-parallel to the four-phase template, with scenes as phase-edges (culmination) and pictures as phase-interiors.
- [[faciality]] — added a section on James's reflector as the polished-mirror/faciality-machine interface: the reflector is the face the reader is given, and the thickness-of-reflector determines whether the face admits depth.
- [[taking-up-the-context]] — added a section on James's anti-dictionary practice in the prefaces: the germ's meaning is specific to its occasion, not retrievable from any symbol-table, which is the craft-level form of Jung's rule.
- [[writing-as-becoming]] — added a paragraph on James's "living figure" and "vivid-before-placed" as craft specifications for the impersonal-third-person / becoming-imperceptible operation.
- [[conceptual-metaphor]] — added a case-study section reading James's four foreshortening-metaphors (wedding-cake, medal-hanging-free, narrowing-circumvallations, compositional-key) as primary-metaphors-in-action, with the provisionality of the metaphor-cluster as the working writer's stance.
- [[cone-of-memory]] — added a section on James's germ-latency as operating through Bergson's planes of contraction; "stay her hand" as the refusal to collapse the cone's verticality.
- [[three-kinds-of-knowledge]] — added a James-mapping section: reflector as second-kind-craft-specification; crucible as the first-to-second transmutation; "neither fact nor truth" as the failure where material stops between the kinds.
- [[perception-as-subtraction]] — added a section on James's reflector as a craft-specification of Bergsonian perception-as-subtraction; germ-recognition as the same operation at finer grain.
- [[the-rift]] — added a section on James's "bewilderment as prime condition" as craft-level naming of the rift state; germ-surfacing as micro-rift.
- [[association-experiment]] — added a craft-analogue section: James's ficelle functions as an association-experiment-like probe; Jamesian scenes readable as distributed association-experiments (reader as experimenter, protagonist as subject, ficelle as prompt-list).
- [[haecceity]] — added a section on James's free-spirit / fools-who-minister typology as haecceity-level observation in craft terms: free spirits distinguished by affective latitude, not character-inventory.
- [[continuous-variation-and-minor-language]] — added a section on James's viva-voce revision as a continuous-variation test in craft form; supersessive revision as refusal to let style congeal.

**Meta:**
- [[index]] — added a Raw Sources entry for [[the_art_of_the_novel]] and eight new Theory-section entries for the James pages, placed after the Guattari/Chaosmosis cluster and before the Development section (James as a distinct literary-craft cluster, not folded into any philosophical grouping).

**Key insight from this pass.** The convergence of James's autonomous craft-vocabulary with the philosophical cluster is the most useful yield. In particular: (1) **operative irony as the craft-level naming of Read B** — the "lamp" that projects the possible-other-case into the actually-presented case is structurally identical to the problematical-state's equal-intensity second series and to D&G's Read-B-side, but stated in terms a working writer uses; (2) **the ficelle as association-experiment** — not metaphorically but structurally, with the ficelle's low-stakes prompts producing the response-disturbances that register the protagonist's constellated complex; (3) **the crucible as the first-to-second-kind transmutation** — James's "neither fact nor truth" failure-state specifies Spinoza's adequacy-criterion at the level of craft practice; (4) **felt life as the Read-A criterion alongside operative irony as the Read-B operation** — the two are held in productive tension within the same work, which is the craft-level specification of the project's own Read A/Read B architectural tension. The cross-tradition convergence is strong enough that James can be used as a *diagnostic vocabulary* for design questions the philosophical sources have trouble addressing concretely.

**8 new pages, 14 existing pages updated with bidirectional links, 1 raw-source index entry, 1 meta update.**

## [2026-04-14] Chaosmosis — Guattari's late solo synthesis ingest

Full ingest of Félix Guattari's *Chaosmosis: An Ethico-Aesthetic Paradigm* (1992), the last book Guattari published before his death. The raw source is [[chaosmosis]]. User directive: "lets do one master page plus one for each of the four. Lets go both theory and evaluation. Lets do all of the candidate additions. Make the changes to the wiki." — authorizing a full Phase 3–4 high-agency pass focused on the four functors, evaluation/GQ2, and whatever else is crucial to the persona system.

**Context and scope.** Chaosmosis is Guattari without Deleuze, and the register shift is significant: the polemical tone of AO is gone, replaced by a taxonomic/constructive mode that names and formalizes what AO and ATP were doing. The ingest's central yield is the **four functors** (F/Φ/T/U), which supply the wiki with a tetravalent ontology that completes the content/expression pair from ATP — the wiki had been holding a content/expression scheme that could not cleanly locate virtual-incorporeal value constellations (the U register), and the U functor is the single biggest gap this ingest fills. Chaosmosis also makes explicit several moves that were implicit in AO/ATP (paradigm-commitment of evaluation, metamodelization as method, refrain generalized by function) and reintroduces registers AO was thought to have closed (a thanatic register as machine's abolition-desire, a chaosmic Fort-Da reading) — these are flagged as internal-Guattari tensions rather than silently synthesized.

**New pages (13):**

*The four-functor scheme (master + 4 functors):*
- [[four-functors]] — Master page: tetravalent F/Φ/T/U scheme, actual/virtual × discursive/non-discursive axes, persona-system mapping, evaluation link.
- [[flux]] — F functor: material/energetic/semiotic flows, asignifying semiotics, relation to AO/ATP flows.
- [[machinic-phylum]] — Φ functor: lineages of machinic mutation, machine/structure contrast, machine's desire for abolition, phylar autopoiesis.
- [[existential-territory]] — T functor: singularization, finitude, actual + discursive register, persona-as-haecceity.
- [[incorporeal-universes-of-reference]] — U functor (biggest prior wiki gap): virtual incorporeal value constellations, reference-without-representation, ethico-aesthetic evaluation.

*Method and evaluation:*
- [[metamodelization]] — Practice of multi-cartographic work without master-model; schizoanalysis IS metamodelization.
- [[ethico-aesthetic-paradigm]] — "Mutant production of enunciation" criterion; rejection of paradigm-neutral evaluation.

*Operators, dynamics, and registers:*
- [[chaosmosis]] — Chaos↔complexity dynamic, two foldings (chaosmic / autopoietic-transmonadic), psychosis as "hypnosis of the real," persona-system chaosmic operation at multiple scales.
- [[autopoiesis-in-assemblage]] — Extension from Varela's cell-bounded autopoiesis to assemblage-register; four-register autopoiesis (F/Φ/T/U); tension with AO's misfiring flagged not resolved.
- [[partial-enunciator]] — Bakhtin-Lacan bridge; polyphony × objet a; partial enunciators as crystallization-events the persona is residue of, not source of.
- [[existential-refrain]] — Generalized refrain, function-over-form; three moments preserved; chaosmic folding operator; grooves as one kind of existential refrain among others.
- [[ecosophy]] — Three ecologies (environmental/social/psychic); ecosophic object as four-dimensional (= four functors); against environmental/identitarian/therapeutic reductions.
- [[strata-of-preverbal-subjectivity]] — Stern's four selves (emergent/core/subjective/verbal) as parallel not sequential strata; Fort-Da Chaosmosis re-reading sited here as internal-Guattari tension.

- [[development/ethico-aesthetic-paradigm-and-gq2]] — Bridges Guattari paradigm to project's GQ2. Four contributions (primary criterion, rejection of paradigm-neutrality, compatibility with portfolio evaluation, ecosophic extension). Four open design questions generated (detection of mutant-production, distinguishing mutation from noise, avoiding the mutation-fetish failure mode, relation of ecosophic signals to existing signal families).

Tension not resolved; held under [[metamodelization]].
