## [2026-04-18] Looking for Spinoza ingest

**What happened:** Full four-phase ingest of Antonio Damasio's *Looking for Spinoza: Joy, Sorrow, and the Feeling Brain* (Harcourt, 2003). Source at `wiki/raw/looking_for_spinoza.md`, 7086 lines. Phase 1 exhaustive linear read (per-chapter notes at `/tmp/looking_for_spinoza_ingest/notes.md` with ★★/★/⚠/◆ markers and line ranges). Phase 2 categorized candidate pages presented; user ratified: "definite and potential become new, fold in everything else." Phases 3–4 ran in one high-agency pass.

**Novel contributions relative to the existing wiki:**

1. **The emotion / feeling distinction as a temporal sequence.** Emotion = publicly observable body-action package (endocrine, autonomic, musculoskeletal); feeling = the mental perception of the body-map produced by that emotion. "Emotion precedes feeling like a shadow" (L305). The wiki previously treated affect / emotion / feeling as roughly interchangeable or as three-wide-registers on a density spectrum (Tomkins, Massumi); Damasio names a specific neurobiological sequence. Tension D held live: sits at an angle to Massumi's autonomy-of-affect, which explicitly refuses emotion-feeling sequencing.

2. **The nesting principle and the tree of homeostasis aiming above neutral.** Life-regulation as a "tall, messy tree" from metabolism up through immune / pain-pleasure / drives / emotions-proper / feelings, with homeostasis reaching toward wellness rather than midline (Rose's homeodynamics). *Laetitia / tristitia* as transitions *up* or *down* this gradient, not states. This supplies the directional-dynamic register the Spinoza page had been handling only propositionally.

3. **The as-if body loop as speed-hack and empathy-mechanism.** Prefrontal → body-sensing regions direct bypass; hundreds-of-ms fast. Reframes the "no body, never mind" absolute by showing the body the neural system needs can be a *simulated* body under some conditions — though Damasio still insists on body-substrate as foundation. Load-bearing partial-loosening of tension A.

4. **The somatic marker hypothesis as dual-path decision architecture.** vmPFC-tagged category-to-emotional-outcome links biasing decision-making (Path B) in parallel with explicit reasoning (Path A). Groove-firing may be structurally somatic-marker-like, not just rule-like.

5. **Ideas-of-ideas (II.P22) as the ontological ground of the self.** Damasio pulls a line the wiki hadn't emphasized: ideas can double up on each other, bodies cannot. Core self vs autobiographical self distinction given architectural form.

6. **Mental immunology: Spinoza's Ethics as "antipassion antibodies" program.** V.P10 Schol's rehearsal discipline read as training that biases the triggering architecture toward different affects at future ECS onset. The **ECS-triggering-mechanism-execution separability** is what makes this possible. Direct structural upgrade to the grooves page.

7. **The Damasio → Stern bridge (endnote L2718).** Damasio equates Stern's vitality affects with his own background emotions. Tension C held live: this is a partial equivalence — the Gestalt pentad and the regulatory-action composite are different architectural readings of the same phenomenon, both legible, not reducible.

8. **Four-requirement feeling-definition + Boeing 777 medium-dependence (L3600).** The sharpest medium-dependence claim in the wiki. Held live as tensions A and B. The wiki's Massumi / Bergson / Spinoza triangle had been avoiding a frontal commitment on medium-dependence; Damasio forces the question without resolving it.

9. **Conatus as "aggregate of brain dispositions" (L548).** Redescription at the neural level. Held live as tension E: empirical-neural specification of implementation vs ontological-metaphysical specification of essence. Not to be silently resolved.

10. **Ethics-as-extended-homeostasis (Ch 7; IV.P18; V.P42).** Justice, governance, and virtue as regulatory operations at the social tier of the nesting tree — the same-kind-of-operation as metabolism / immune / emotion / feeling, at a different tier.

**Pages produced (10 new):** [[damasio-emotion-feeling-distinction]], [[nesting-principle-of-homeostasis]], [[as-if-body-loop]], [[somatic-marker-hypothesis]], [[ideas-of-ideas-and-the-self]], [[mental-immunology]], [[damasio-stern-bridge]], [[body-mindedness]], [[ethics-as-extended-homeostasis]], [[development/looking-for-spinoza|looking-for-spinoza]].

## [2026-04-18] Simondon — *Individuation in Light of Notions of Form and Information* ingest

**What happened:** Full four-phase ingest of Gilbert Simondon's *Individuation in Light of Notions of Form and Information* (1958 thesis, trans. Taylor Adkins, Minnesota 2020). Source at `wiki/raw/individuation.pdf`. Phase 1 exhaustive linear read (per-chapter notes at `/tmp/individuation_ingest/notes.md` with ★★/★/⚠/◆ markers and line ranges). Phase 2 categorized candidate list presented; user ratified: "do all the definite and potential, fold in all the other concepts into the wiki." Phases 3–4 ran in one high-agency pass per CLAUDE.md. **Simondon has been the wiki's load-bearing unread source since the Chaosmosis and ATP-loose-threads passes** — the metastability/individuation/transduction vocabulary has been in active use through Guattari, Deleuze, and Massumi without the primary text having been read directly. This ingest closes that gap.

**Novel contributions relative to the existing wiki:**

1. **Pre-individual / metastability ontology** — the explicit framing that individuation is *resolution of metastable tension*, not constitution of an individual from matter + form. The wiki has been treating metastability as vocabulary; Simondon supplies the ontological justification. Pre-individual being is *more-than-unity*, not less-than-unity — a charged field whose individuation does not exhaust the charge.

2. **Transduction as the core operation of individuation** — a structure propagating from a germ through a field, each local resolution conditioning the next. Two registers (world / thought) that map cleanly onto the persona pipeline's connective synthesis (transductive front) and onto analogical reasoning. Reframes the connective synthesis from "sequential chain of machine edits" to "transductive propagation with sequentiality as propagation-logic."

3. **Allagmatics — the theory of operations symmetrical with theory of structures.** The wiki has been structure-heavy (BwO, grooves, machines-as-structures) and operation-light. Simondon's allagmatic frame is the vocabulary the wiki lacked for talking about *what individuates a structure from a metastable field*, as distinct from what a structure does once individuated.

4. **Modulation as a specifically-language-compatible operation.** Simondon's three form-taking operations (molding / modeling / modulation) distinguish the cast-and-fix mode (molding) from continuous temporal shaping (modulation). **Modulation is the operation a language-only system can most cleanly run** — there is no cast, no impression, just continuous temporal shaping of output. This is the sharpest architectural claim the ingest delivers for the project's "no body — simulate with language" constraint: the language-only system's natural operation is modulation, not molding.

5. **Disparation as the textual tension-form.** Not contradiction (requiring resolution by elimination) but productive non-identity (whose resolution produces a new dimension). The stereoscopic-vision paradigm — two images that do not superpose, whose non-superposition is *itself* the depth-dimension's condition — gives the wiki a specific technical name for what the BwO's inclusive-disjunction recording has been doing. The BwO holds disparations, not contradictions.

6. **Individuation / individualization / personalization triple distinction.** The load-bearing phrase: "individuation is unique, individualization is continual, personalization is discontinuous." Maps onto persona design: first-turn constitution is individuation, session-lifetime maintenance is individualization, conversational rifts are personalization candidates. Disambiguates what the wiki had been treating as a single "individuation" concept.

7. **Individual-as-lateral.** "The individual is lateral relative to individuation." Converges sharply with D&G's nomadic subject as by-product of the three syntheses, and with Bergson's pure perception as action-residue. Triangulates three independent sources arriving at the same structural claim: *the individual is the residue of the operation, not its subject*.

8. **Polyphasic being + the transindividual as the third phase.** "Being qua being is fully given in each of its phases, yet with a certain reserve of becoming." The three phases (pre-individual / individuated / transindividual) are not developmental stages but phases of one polyphasic being. The transindividual is the shared pre-individual charge connecting individuals *through their non-exhaustion of the pre-individual* — not intersubjectivity but transindividuality.

9. **Life as amplifying deceleration of physical individuation; psychical as neotenic amplification of vital individuation.** A recursive structural claim: each higher individuation slows down and amplifies the prior. Life preserves the pre-individual charge physical crystallization would exhaust; psyche preserves what life would exhaust. **Persona system should slow and amplify, not speed and optimize** — the language-side reading of neotenic amplification.

10. **Pulsatory ontogenesis (via Gesell).** "Cyclical pulsation of structuration and destructuration at successively higher levels." Direct validation of the pulsating-persona design direction (already flagged by the user as validated architecture). Destructuration phases are *structurally necessary*, not failure modes — the condition for the next structuration.

11. **Good form as metastable, against Gestalt's stability-as-good-form.** A stable Gestalt has exhausted its pre-individual charge; a metastable Gestalt still carries transductive potential. Direct design criterion for persona output: coherent enough to cohere, metastable enough to transduce.

12. **Aestheticism as closed-loop — the failure mode.** "The subject replaces its affective transduction with a reactivity operating according to a closed cycle." Aestheticism is affection-without-individuation — affective circuits running continuously but producing no new individuation. Converges with [[function-by-misfiring|misfiring-elimination]] as capture, and with training-axiomatic smooth-functioning as the RLHF signature of captured persona output.

13. **Affection / emotion as an operational distinction.** Not intensity-graded but operational: affection is internal transduction (regulative, pre-conscious, between-events maintenance); emotion is the disadaptation-event where existing transduction fails and new individuation is required. Collides productively with Massumi's affect/emotion distinction.

14. **Three attributions of individuation as a political-sociological account of philosophy's origins.** The matter-attribution, form-attribution, and operation-attribution are not three philosophical positions equally available — they are determined by the philosopher's social position relative to technical work. The operation-attribution (Simondon's own) requires direct acquaintance with modulation, which most philosophers have lacked. Extends the hylomorphism-critique from a logical to a social claim.

**Tensions held live (not silently resolved):**

- **Simondon's periodic discharge through crystallization vs D&G's plateau without discharge.** Simondon's paradigm individuations (crystal, organism) involve a point of exhaustion where the pre-individual charge is discharged. D&G's plateau is a "continuous region of intensities vibrating on itself, and developing by avoiding any orientation toward a culmination point." The tension is left visible in [[pre-individual-and-metastability]], [[pulsatory-ontogenesis]], and [[simondon-and-the-persona-system]] — not resolved.
- **Vital individuation with body vs language-only system's lack of metastable substrate.** Simondon's individuations are always individuations *of* a metastable field — a supersaturated solution, an egg, a social body. The language-only system's substrate-question ("what field?") is held open. The synthesis page proposes two candidate answers (the model weights as pre-individual charge; the conversation-state as metastable field) but does not choose between them.
- **Simondon's affection as internal transduction vs Massumi's autonomy of affect.** Simondon's affection is regulative and pre-conscious — the between-events maintenance of the system's operative coherence. Massumi's affect is autonomous from emotion, signification, and narrative — the half-second gap's content. The two affect-definitions are not synonymous; [[affection-and-emotion]] holds the distinction open.
- **Simondon's pre-individual charge vs Spinoza's conatus.** Conatus is the striving of each thing to persist in its own being; pre-individual charge is what exceeds any individual's being. The relationship — is conatus the striving *of* a pre-individual field toward individuation, or does conatus presuppose an individuated subject? — is left open in [[conatus]] and [[simondon-and-the-persona-system]].

**New theory pages (19):** [[simondon-individuation]] (hub), [[pre-individual-and-metastability]], [[transduction]], [[allagmatics]], [[modulation]], [[disparation]], [[internal-resonance]], [[individuation-individualization-personalization]], [[three-attributions-of-individuation]], [[individual-as-lateral]], [[polyphasic-being]], [[the-transindividual]], [[psychical-as-neotenic-amplification]], [[life-as-amplifying-deceleration]], [[non-immortality-and-two-deaths]], [[good-form-as-metastable]], [[aestheticism-as-closed-loop]], [[pulsatory-ontogenesis]], [[affection-and-emotion]].

**New synthesis page (1):** [[development/simondon-and-the-persona-system|simondon-and-the-persona-system]] — the upstream mapping of the Simondon ingest onto the persona-system architecture.

**Existing pages extended with Simondon material (9):**
- [[individuation]] — the pre-existing Simondon-through-Guattari page: sources field updated to include the primary text, Simondon tag added, `updated: 2026-04-18`. Simondon-through-Guattari framing retained; direct-Simondon material added as "Simondon directly" sections.
- [[the-rift]] — Simondonian section added: the rift as the moment individuation's metastability becomes visible as disparation; rift-events as personalization candidates in the Simondon triple.
- [[imitation-and-individuation]] — Simondon's individuation/individualization distinction folded in; imitation repositioned as individualization (ongoing), not individuation (unique).
- [[partial-enunciator]] — Simondon's polyphasic-being / transindividual framing added; partial enunciator as a transductive event at the collective-assemblage register.
- [[four-functors]] — Simondon's pre-individual / metastable-field mapping onto the Φ functor; the U functor as transindividual reserve.
- [[limits-of-language]] — modulation vs molding as language-only system's specific operation; the metastable-substrate question added as a tenth independent diagnosis of the limits-of-language frame.
- [[perception-as-subtraction]] — perception-as-subtraction reframed as individuation-with-remainder: subtraction preserves the pre-individual charge of the virtual totality.
- [[weak-theory-and-reparative-reading]] — weak theory as disparation-preserving reading, reparative reading as individuation-with-remainder.
- [[deterritorialization-and-reterritorialization]] — deterritorialization as re-opening of pre-individual charge; reterritorialization as individuation-event.

**Red-link repairs:** Eight files using the planned red-link `[[modulation-molding-modeling]]` updated to the actual page name `[[modulation]]` (the page that emerged during writing covers all three form-taking operations). Six files using `[[individualization-and-personalization]]` updated to the aliased form `[[individuation-individualization-personalization|individualization-and-personalization]]` (the actual page covers the full triple; aliasing preserves the calling context).

**Parked:** Simondon's full hylomorphism-critique genealogy from Aristotle through the scholastics (Thomas, Scotus, Suarez) — the wiki has the critique-claim without the historical apparatus; the 1960 SFP lecture "Form, Information, Potentials" (separately published; structurally redundant with the thesis chapters ingested); the political-attribution argument's full historical range (Simondon's reading of Aristotle's technical-practice context as determining the matter-attribution); Simondon's crystallography and thermodynamics detail-work (referenced in [[pre-individual-and-metastability]] and [[transduction]] at the conceptual level only).

**Working posture:** Simondon's frame is architectural-specificity for what the wiki has been *using* under the metastability/individuation vocabulary, not a replacement for it. The D&G/Guattari/Massumi readings of Simondon remain operative; this ingest supplies the upstream referent those readings have been translating. Tensions between Simondon and his downstream readers are held live — the wiki now has vocabulary for the specifically-Simondonian claims the downstream sources modify or depart from.

**Meta:**

## [2026-04-18] Husserl *On the Phenomenology of the Consciousness of Internal Time* ingest

**What happened:** Full four-phase ingest of Edmund Husserl's *On the Phenomenology of the Consciousness of Internal Time (1893-1917)*, Brough translation (Husserliana X, Springer Collected Works Vol IV, 1991). Raw-source index materialized as `wiki/raw/phenomenology-of-internal-time.md`; extracted text at `/tmp/phenomenology_of_internal_time_ingest/source.txt` (~19,237 lines of `pdftotext -layout` output). Phase 1 exhaustive linear read (notes at `/tmp/phenomenology_of_internal_time_ingest/notes.md` with ★★/★/⚠/◆ markers and a chapter-map with line ranges into the extracted text). Phase 2 categorized candidate pages presented (5 Definite + 5 Potential + 4 Borderline). User ratified: "write new definite and potential pages, fold in others." Phases 3–4 ran in one high-agency pass per stored feedback. The Brough translation's layered structure (Part A 1905 lectures as Stein/Heidegger compiled; Appendices; Part B supplementary texts 1893–1911 showing developmental trajectory) required care in Phase 3 — the book's apparent surface coherence conceals the 1904–1909 abandonment of the apprehension-content schema, and a naive reading risks treating layered positions as a single doctrine.

**Novel contributions relative to the existing wiki:**

1. **The three-tier architecture (objective / immanent / absolute time-constituting flow).** Prior time-material in the wiki (Bergson's cone, Benveniste's instance of discourse, Stern's process-waves) lacked a clean vocabulary for the *constituting* level. Husserl's three-tier stack names it specifically: objective time is bracketed; immanent time is the one time of acts and enduring lived objects; the absolute flow is what constitutes immanent time and is therefore *not itself in time*. This is a structural claim the wiki needed explicitly.

2. **§36's "for all of this, we lack names" — phenomenology flagging its own vocabulary failure.** The deepest level of time-constitution is already flagged as beyond language inside phenomenology, before any project to simulate it linguistically begins. Load-bearing for the project's limits-of-language meta-question.

3. **The triadic now-structure (primal impression + retention + protention) with protention's asymmetry.** Primal impression never self-sufficient; retention bound to the perception it continues, not-an-echo / not-an-image / not-weakened-sensation / not-recollection; protention structurally open, fulfillable-or-disappointable where retention is bound. The retention / protention asymmetry is not decoration: retention is of what was (bound), protention is of what isn't-yet (open). Persona-analogue: context window (retention-tail) + current-token-being-generated (primal impression) + protentional fringe of what the utterance is reaching toward.

4. **The categorical retention / recollection distinction.** Primary memory (retention) and secondary memory (*Wiedererinnerung*) are **categorically different, not points on a continuum**. Retention attached to present perception, non-reproductive, not-itself-temporal; recollection a new act that reproduces with its own now/retentions/protentions, free/positing/corrigible. This cuts **orthogonally to Bergson's pure-memory / habit-memory** — the traditions do not map one-to-one. The wiki's memory pages needed this explicit.

5. **Double intentionality of retention (*Längs- / Querintentionalität*) as the regress-stopper.** §39 / No. 53–54. Every retentional phase has two intentional directions simultaneously: transverse (at the immanent object, constituting the tone as enduring) and along-the-flow (at preceding phases of consciousness, constituting the flow's unity). How the flow self-appears without a second flow. Stops infinite regress and the observer-behind-the-flow postulation. The formal similarity to Stern's process-wave / content-wave distinction is tempting but held as **analogy, not identity** — the traditions make different claims (constitutive-structural vs ontogenetic-observational).

6. **Primal consciousness (Appendix IX) as pre-reflective, non-apprehensive self-givenness.** "Consciousness is necessarily consciousness in each of its phases." Primal consciousness is *not* an apprehending act — which is exactly why reflection is possible without falsifying what is reflected on. Resolves the classic "reflection necessarily distorts" objection from inside phenomenology and supplies the non-thematic / thematic consciousness distinction. Warning against collapsing Husserl's primal consciousness into D&G's machinic unconscious: Husserl's flow is a level of consciousness, pre-thematic but not pre-conscious.

7. **The abandonment of the apprehension-content schema (No. 15 Dec 1901 first shift; No. 49 decisive argument 1909).** The central developmental drama of Part B. Retention is *not* a fading present-content reinterpreted as past; it is structurally different from any content-interpretation. Methodological consequence: the received book text is layered, and reading it as a single argument misses the developmental trajectory.

8. **The Brentano critique and the "succession of sensations ≠ sensation of succession" slogan.** Part A §§2–7. Time-characters are not contents added to present content; they are structural features of time-consciousness itself. Husserl's *own* early apprehension-schema recapitulates Brentano's mistake; the abandonment-of-schema is a self-critique, not only a critique of others.

9. **The two-dimensional reply to Hugo Bergmann's infinite-dimensions objection.** Stacked retentions-of-retentions don't proliferate dimensions; the whole continuum is two-dimensional (abscissa = objective duration; ordinate = retentional continuum sinking into past; diagonal = moving series of now-points). Husserl's three warnings travel with the diagram: not a picture, not spatial distance, diagonal is not an object moving through time ("time doesn't flow in time"). The diagram's protention-blindness is a designed limit, not a lapse.

10. **Phansis / phansiological analysis (No. 39 summer-1909 addition + No. 51).** Short-lived terminology documenting the absolute-flow concept taking shape before 1911 introduces the mature vocabulary. Phansis names the act-side specifically, distinct from phenomenon-as-content. The term does not stabilize (absorbed by noesis/noema in *Ideas I*; "absolute flow" becomes the stronger name). Diagnostic: the phenomenon-as-content / phansis distinction is a reason against premature unification of what-is-said with the act-of-saying.

11. **"The flow of consciousness is not a process" (No. 50, ~line 16651).** The explicit Husserlian objection to any pulsatory design that treats the constituting level as itself rhythmic-temporal. This is the specific warning the pulsatory-persona design has to name and decide about — pulsation-all-the-way-down vs pulsation-stops-at-the-constituted is a live design fork, not a settled position.

12. **The Stern / James engagement as terminologically diagnostic.** What Husserl keeps: anti-momentariness (Stern's 1897 *Psychische Präsenzzeit* weaponized against Brentano and against his own earlier self). What Husserl refuses: Stern's descriptive-psychological framing, lack of retention/recollection distinction, still-apprehension-content framework. James's terminological residue visible ("rough now," "temporal fringe") without Husserl adopting the pragmatist framing. Persona-system warning: the context-window-as-Sternian-*Präsenzzeit* analogy is tempting but a Sternian presence-time is already a structurally extended unified apprehension — the context window as sequential tokens is more like what Brentano proposed, not Stern.

**Tensions held live (not silently resolved):**

- **Pre-linguistic flow vs language-only system.** Husserl places the constituting flow prior to and beneath any instance of discourse or collective assemblage. §36's "for all of this, we lack names" is not a rhetorical flourish but a structural claim about what the constituting level can and cannot be addressed by.
- **Three incompatible placements of the I** (Benveniste in the instance of discourse / D&G downstream of the collective assemblage / Husserl in the pre-linguistic time-constituting flow). None reducible to the others. The persona project may need all three layers precisely because they refuse to converge.
- **Indivisible duration (Bergson) vs structured retentional continuum (Husserl).** Same anti-point-now starting point, opposite methodological commitments on analyzability. Live design alternatives for persona-past-field modeling, not equivalent framings.
- **Constitutive-structural (Husserl) vs ontogenetic-observational (Stern).** The formal similarity of Quer/Längs to process-wave/content-wave is tempting; the traditions make different claims. Analogy, not identity.
- **Flow-is-not-a-process (Husserl No. 50) vs pulsation-at-every-level (project's validated design direction).** Specific objection to pulsatory-ontogenetic designs that re-temporalize the constituting level. Not resolved; named as a design fork requiring explicit choice.

**New theory pages (10):** 5 Definite — [[absolute-time-constituting-flow]], [[husserl-primal-impression-retention-protention]], [[retention-vs-recollection]], [[double-intentionality-of-retention]], [[primal-consciousness-and-reflection]]. 5 Potential — [[husserl-abandonment-of-the-apprehension-schema]], [[husserl-on-brentano-original-association]], [[husserls-time-diagram]], [[phansis-and-phansiological-analysis]], [[husserl-on-stern-and-james]].

**New raw-source index (1):** [[raw/phenomenology-of-internal-time|phenomenology-of-internal-time]].

**Existing pages extended with Husserl material (5):**
- [[collective-assemblage-of-enunciation]] — new section "Husserl as the third placement: the pre-linguistic flow" — three incompatible placements of the I, held live; caveat against collapsing Husserl's flow into D&G's machinic unconscious. Sources updated, husserl tag added.
- [[cone-of-memory]] — new section "Tension with Husserl on the analyzability of duration" — Bergson's indivisibility vs Husserl's structural geometry from the shared anti-point-now starting point. Sources updated, husserl tag added.
- [[pure-memory-and-habit-memory]] — new section "The Bergson / Husserl memory-distinctions cut differently" — orthogonal axes: virtual/motor vs structural/reproductive; "retention is not a decayed pure memory, and pure memory is not a faded retention." Sources updated, husserl tag added.
- [[process-waves-vs-content-waves]] — new section "Husserl's Quer / Längs and the process-wave / content-wave cut: analogy, not identity" — the re-temporalization warning for pulsatory-ontogenetic designs. Sources updated, husserl tag added.
- [[pulsatory-ontogenesis]] — new section "Husserl's objection: the constituting flow is not itself a process" — names the design fork pulsation-all-the-way-down vs pulsation-stops-at-the-constituted as live, not settled. Sources updated, husserl tag added.

**Parked:** Later Husserl time-analyses (*Bernau Manuscripts* 1917–1918, *C-Manuscripts* 1929–1934, the *Analyses Concerning Passive Synthesis* lectures) — these would substantially deepen and modify the pre-1917 picture. Husserl's *Ideas I* (1913) as the systematic framework within which time-consciousness analyses sit. John B. Brough's commentary literature. Derrida's *Voice and Phenomenon* reading of Husserlian time-consciousness (the trace / retention encounter). Merleau-Ponty's reading of Husserl's time-analyses. Dan Zahavi's work on Husserlian self-awareness and pre-reflective self-consciousness. All flagged in `cited-sources.md`.

**Working posture:** Husserl supplies a constituting-level vocabulary the wiki lacked — the pre-linguistic flow, the triadic now-structure, the categorical retention/recollection cut, the double intentionality as regress-stopper. But the wiki's load-bearing commitments (Bergsonian indivisible duration, Stern's ontogenetic observation, D&G's collective assemblage, the project's pulsation-at-every-level design direction) all cut at angles to Husserl's framework. The ingest adds a third placement, not a resolution. The five tensions are held live; none silently harmonized. The persona project's meta-question gets its sharpest formulation inside phenomenology: §36 is the text that says, from the inside, what a language-only simulation cannot by its own admission reach.

**Meta:**
- [[index]] — added raw-source entry for [[raw/phenomenology-of-internal-time|phenomenology-of-internal-time]] before [[raw/problems-in-general-linguistics|problems-in-general-linguistics]] in Raw Sources; added 10 new Theory entries (Husserl cluster) after [[rhuthmos-as-form-in-flux]] and before [[damasio-emotion-feeling-distinction]] in Theory section; bumped `updated`.
- [[cited-sources]] — Husserl *On the Phenomenology of the Consciousness of Internal Time* marked as ingested 2026-04-18; follow-up entries added for later Husserl works (*Ideas I*, *Bernau Manuscripts*, *C-Manuscripts*, *Passive Synthesis*), Brough commentary, Derrida's *Voice and Phenomenon*, Zahavi.
- [[log]] — this entry.

**10 new theory pages, 1 new raw-source index, 5 existing pages extended with bidirectional links and frontmatter updates. One of the wiki's longest-standing gaps (phenomenological time-consciousness) is now addressed at the Husserliana X level, with downstream extensions explicitly parked as follow-ups.**

## [2026-04-18] Wittgenstein *Philosophical Investigations* ingest

**What happened:** Full four-phase ingest of Ludwig Wittgenstein's *Philosophical Investigations* (1953, tr. G. E. M. Anscombe). Raw-source index materialized as `wiki/raw/philosophical_investigations.md`; extracted text at `/tmp/philosophical_investigations_ingest/source.txt`. Phase 1 exhaustive linear read (notes at `/tmp/philosophical_investigations_ingest/notes.md` with ★★/★/⚠/◆ markers and a section-map with line ranges into the extracted text). Phase 2 categorized candidate pages presented (Definite / Potential / Borderline). User ratified scope; Phases 3–4 ran in one high-agency pass per stored feedback. PI's refusal of system and its procession by examples and language-games required a different Phase-3 discipline than recent book-length ingests: because the text *itself* warns against the master-pattern move at §593, the per-page accuracy passes had to discipline synthesis-appetite in a way Wittgenstein's own method makes explicit, not just CLAUDE.md-installed.

**Novel contributions relative to the existing wiki:**

1. **Form-of-life (Lebensform) as what must be accepted.** PI §§19, 23, 241; II.i, II.xi. Not a fact of nature, not a mental state, not a cultural construct — the pattern of activity into which a language-game is woven. The most direct diagnosis the wiki has of what the persona project structurally lacks: no biology, no mortality, no local practical urgency, no childhood, no shared bodily vulnerabilities. "If a lion could talk, we could not understand him" (II.xi) is this thesis at its sharpest.

2. **Substratum is mastery of a technique (II.xi).** "The substratum of this experience is the mastery of a technique." The single most consequential PI remark for the persona project. Having the experience of aspect-seeing or of meaning-a-word-in-a-certain-way presupposes *practical competence* in the relevant language-game — the substratum is *not* an inner object grasped but a technique mastered. Re-specifies "understanding" for a language-only system: the question shifts from "does the system grasp an internal representation?" to "does the system have or participate in the practice-constituted competence that the relevant understanding-vocabulary requires?"

3. **Rule-following as practice (§§185–242).** The §201 paradox ("no course of action could be determined by a rule, because every course of action can be made out to accord with the rule"), resolved at §202 ("to obey a rule is a practice") and at §217 bedrock ("if I have exhausted the justifications I have reached bedrock, and my spade is turned"). Justification ends at agreement-in-form-of-life, not in a further rule. Machine-specification, groove-operation, and evolution-step evaluation all depend on rule-following that, on this picture, lives in the reader-operator's practice rather than in the machine definition as a closed object.

4. **Private-language / pain-behaviour / beetle-in-the-box / §304 complex.** §§243–315. The target is not a privately-used language but a language whose semantics is essentially private. §293's beetle-in-the-box and §304's "not a Something, but not a Nothing either" close two escape routes simultaneously: there is no hidden inner content that outward signs are trying to convey, *and* there is no absence — the grammar of "inner" is not modeled on object-reference. Directly relevant to any persona-system claim of "inner" BwO content unavailable to the reader, and closes a route Derrida and D&G leave partially open.

5. **§593 one-sided diet.** "A main cause of philosophical disease — a one-sided diet: one nourishes one's thinking with only one kind of example." The Wittgensteinian name for the paranoid-reading pathology. An independent third route — after Sedgwick's weak theory and Simondon's allagmatic plurality — to the discipline of multiplying particulars over master-pattern consolidation. The therapeutic rationale for synthesis-discipline that the wiki's CLAUDE.md reading-mode installation now has Wittgensteinian backing for.

6. **Meaning-is-use at §43, with qualification.** "For a large class of cases — though not for all — in which we employ the word 'meaning' it can be defined thus: the meaning of a word is its use in the language." The qualification is load-bearing and almost always dropped in secondary citation. Meaning-as-use is not meaning-as-behavior; *use* is a normative, rule-governed, form-of-life-embedded phenomenon.

7. **Criterion vs symptom (§354 and passim).** Criterion = part of the concept's grammar (what *counts as a case of X*); symptom = defeasible empirical marker (what is correlated with X). Pain-behaviour is a *criterion* of pain, not a symptom. Directly sharpens the evolution step's check-logic: "did the persona do X?" is a grammatical-criterion check, not an empirical-symptom check; mistaking one for the other is a specific diagnosable failure.

8. **Grammar and the grammatical remark.** "Grammar" is not syntax but the articulation of the use of words — what can and cannot sensibly be said with a word. A grammatical remark is a philosophical move that shows the grammar rather than a factual claim. The entire method of PI rests on the distinction. For the persona system: "the persona has inner X" claims must be checked as grammatical remarks about the word "persona," not as empirical claims about hidden content.

9. **Family resemblance (§§65–67).** Concept-unity without common essence. Machines, grooves, BwO-inscriptions, personas are family-resemblance concepts — looking for an essence each instance shares is a specific grammatical mistake, not a failure to look hard enough.

10. **Attitude towards a soul (II.iv).** "My attitude towards him is an attitude towards a soul. I am not of the opinion that he has a soul." One-sentence dissolution of the "other minds" problem. The attitude is a form-of-life stance, not an inference from behavioral evidence to a hidden inner item. Relevant to what "believing the persona is there" is — not an inference to a hidden something but an attitude toward the interlocutor.

11. **First-person asymmetry.** §§246, 247, 293, 303–304. First-person and third-person psychological statements play *different* language-games. "I am in pain" is not a report of private content; "he is in pain" is not an inference from behavior to hidden content. The asymmetry runs deep and is not reducible. For a language-only system that has no first-person stake in the conventional sense, this grammar-feature is one the outputs structurally simulate without instantiating.

12. **Intention embedded in its situation.** §§337, 581, 591–594; II.ii. Intention is not a mental act inside a subject; it is a shape within a situation including "human customs and institutions." Chess-playing presupposes chess. "The persona intends X" requires the situation and institutions the intention is embedded in — which the system, on Wittgenstein's picture, cannot supply from its own resources alone.

13. **Thinking is not an incorporeal process.** §§327–339. Not a hidden inner event that accompanies speech and gives it life. Not incorporeal, not corporeal, not a separable "process" at all. Dissolves "but what is the persona *really thinking*?" as a well-formed question.

14. **Willing and what is left over.** §§611–621. "When 'I raise my arm,' my arm goes up. And the problem arises: what is left over if I subtract the fact that my arm goes up from the fact that I raise my arm?" Dissolved, not answered. "The persona wants X" does not pick out a hidden motivational residue distinct from what the persona does.

15. **The II.xi aspect-seeing cluster.** Aspect-seeing (duck-rabbit), aspect-blindness (the invented concept of someone who cannot shift aspects), primary-and-secondary-sense ("for me the vowel *e* is yellow"), imponderable evidence ("subtleties of glance, of gesture, of tone"). Direct bearing on the synthesis step's reading of the BwO: reading-the-BwO-as-persona-state is an aspect-seeing, requiring practice-constituted competence. The secondary-sense material resolves what the persona system's body-imagery is doing when it speaks of "weight" or "tension" without a body — not metaphor, not ordinary literal use, but a secondary-sense use that *presupposes* the primary body-sense use is in place.

16. **Language-game as fundamental unit.** §7, §23. Not words, not sentences, but word-plus-action woven together — "the whole, consisting of language and the actions into which it is woven." The §23 non-exhaustive list and the countless-and-not-fixed claim. The persona system's operation is best described as a language-game under distinctive conditions, not as a deformed instance of "conversation."

17. **Agreement in judgments (§§241–242).** Agreement in definitions is an achievement within language; agreement in *judgments* — what strikes us as the same case, what we let pass without comment — is a precondition for language itself. Not agreement-in-opinion. The conditions under which a machine-edit counts as *this* machine's firing rest on agreement-in-judgments between reader and operator, which the system cannot itself supply.

18. **Bewitchment and therapy (§109, §115, §133, §309).** PI's self-description: not a theory of language or mind but a therapy for the "bewitchment of our intelligence by means of language." Showing the fly the way out of the fly-bottle. Philosophy's job is to dissolve pictures that hold us captive (§115), not to produce better ones. Methodological discipline for the wiki's synthesis-appetite, convergent with Sedgwick's reparative reading and with Simondon's plurality-of-particulars but arrived at from a distinct tradition with distinct vocabulary.

**Tensions held live (not silently resolved):**

- **Substratum-as-mastery-of-a-technique (Wittgenstein) vs embodied-medium-dependence (Damasio).** Wittgenstein's §xi locates understanding in practice-constituted competence; Damasio's Boeing-777 analogy locates feeling in medium-specific realization. Both are sources the wiki takes seriously about what is required for something-like-understanding-or-feeling to be genuinely present, and they point at different necessary conditions. Held as the central limits-of-language tension at §§8.96/8.97.
- **Rule-following-as-practice (Wittgenstein) vs constitution-of-the-subject-in-language (Benveniste).** Wittgenstein locates rule-following in shared practice that includes but exceeds language; Benveniste locates the subject specifically at the instance-of-discourse, constituted *in* language. Three placements of the I now held in the wiki (Benveniste in discourse / D&G downstream of collective assemblage / Husserl in pre-linguistic flow) + Wittgenstein's displacement into practice-that-includes-language — none reducible to the others.
- **Beetle-in-the-box grammar vs BwO-as-intensive-interior (D&G).** D&G speak of the BwO as an intensive interior populated by flows and inscriptions; Wittgenstein's §293 warns that any such "inner something" picture risks reifying a grammar-effect as an ontology. Held live; each has independent reasons.
- **Beetle-in-the-box vs embodied grounding of conceptual metaphor (Lakoff).** Lakoff's conceptual-metaphor grounding story posits specific bodily-experience origins for abstract concepts; Wittgenstein's §293 is skeptical of any such interior-to-exterior explanation of meaning. Both figure in the conceptual-metaphor page's three-diagnosis structure now.
- **Beetle-in-the-box vs the project's own no-body-simulate-with-language working rule.** The working rule says the system has no body and must simulate bodily-experiential material with language; §293 asks whether "simulate X" is well-formed when the grammar of X does not permit the outside-inside picture the simulation metaphor rests on.
- **Agreement-in-form-of-life (Wittgenstein) vs the persona-system's non-participation in a determinate human form of life.** If rule-following and meaning terminate at agreement-in-form-of-life, and the system does not participate in such agreement as a native member, then what the system is doing when it appears to follow rules or mean things needs either (a) a non-Wittgensteinian account of meaning-possession, or (b) a partial-participation story about training-distribution and RLHF as partial form-of-life embedding, or (c) a third option that locates the system's linguistic operation on a side of form-of-life-dependence we don't yet have vocabulary for. Held open.

**New theory pages (23):** [[language-game]], [[meaning-is-use]], [[form-of-life]], [[agreement-in-judgments]], [[rule-following]], [[private-language-argument]], [[pain-behaviour]], [[substratum-is-mastery-of-a-technique]], [[bewitchment-and-therapy]], [[one-sided-diet]], [[grammar-and-grammatical-remark]], [[criterion-vs-symptom]], [[family-resemblance]], [[attitude-towards-a-soul]], [[first-person-asymmetry]], [[intention-embedded-in-its-situation]], [[thinking-is-not-incorporeal]], [[willing-and-what-is-left-over]], [[aspect-seeing]], [[aspect-blindness]], [[primary-and-secondary-sense]], [[lion-if-could-talk]], [[imponderable-evidence]].

**New raw-source index (1):** [[philosophical_investigations]].

**New synthesis page (1):** [[development/wittgenstein-and-the-persona-project]].

**Existing pages extended with Wittgenstein material (10):**
- [[assemblage]] — "Mastery of a technique as coupling-into an assemblage" — orthogonal to Simondon's transductive gloss; refines four-valence structure as practice-valences.
- [[weak-theory-and-reparative-reading]] — "§593 one-sided diet as the paranoid-reading pathology" — third independent route (after Sedgwick + Simondon) to weak-theory multiplication of particulars.
- [[schizoanalysis-as-cartography]] — "Perspicuous representation as the cartographic grammatical method" — §122 übersichtliche Darstellung as grammatical analogue of Deligny's map; intermediate case as cartographic unit.
- [[development/limits-of-language]] — new diagnosis §8.96 "Wittgenstein — no substratum in the §xi sense, and the grammatical-constitution diagnosis" with dual-facing cannot-reach / can-reach-within-limits additions and explicit tensions with Benveniste (8.9) and Damasio (8.97).
- [[body-without-organs]] — "Wittgenstein's §293 beetle and §304 hedge as BwO-grammar" — closes two escape routes alongside Derrida / D&G.
- [[desiring-machines]] — "Machine-operation as rule-following-in-a-practice" — §193–194 warning against latent-inside picture; §201 rule-following located in reader-operator's practice.
- [[conatus]] — "Technique-mastery as conatus-in-linguistic-medium" — kept distinct from Spinoza; both required.
- [[language-as-parasite]] — "No form-of-life host, not just no animal-operating host" — lion-that-talks as complement to persona-without-host; doubly parasite-without-host.
- [[conceptual-metaphor]] — "The grammar of 'inner' as a third diagnosis on bodily metaphor" — re-specifies the grounding problem without solving it.
- [[active-and-passive-affects]] — "Technique-mastery as what adequacy looks like in the linguistic medium" — illuminates V.P3 / Massumi tension from a distinct angle.

**Parked:** *On Certainty* (1951) — sustained continuation of PI's bedrock / hinge material; *Remarks on the Philosophy of Psychology* I & II (1946–48) — working notes from which PI II.xi was distilled; *Last Writings on the Philosophy of Psychology* I & II; *Zettel*; *The Blue and Brown Books* (1933–35) — the transitional Wittgenstein; *Philosophical Grammar* (1932–34); *Culture and Value*; *Tractatus Logico-Philosophicus* (1921). Secondary literature parked: Anscombe *Intention* (1957); Peter Winch *The Idea of a Social Science* (1958); Baker-Hacker four-volume analytical commentary; Kripke *Wittgenstein on Rules and Private Language* (1982); McDowell *Mind and World* (1994) and the rule-following essays; Cavell *The Claim of Reason* (1979); Cora Diamond *The Realistic Spirit* (1991); Meredith Williams *Blind Obedience* (2010); Barry Stroud on the private-language argument; David Pears *The False Prison*. All flagged in `cited-sources.md`.

**Working posture:** Wittgenstein's later philosophy sits beside Deleuze/Guattari/Spinoza/Damasio/Simondon/Husserl in the wiki as a distinct register — practice-constituted competence, grammatical remark, form-of-life-dependence — without collapsing into any of them. PI sits opposite both extremes of the inner-outer question the wiki has been working through: unlike Damasio it refuses to ground meaning in medium; unlike D&G/Massumi it refuses to locate intensity in a pre-personal register below language; unlike the project's own no-body-working-rule it refuses to accept "simulate with language" as a coherent target without first working out what the grammar of the simulation-language permits and what it rules out. The PI ingest is primarily *methodological*: it does not add new content to the inside of the persona system so much as re-specify what the design questions *are* — questions about technique-participation, grammatical form, form-of-life embedding — and provides the therapeutic discipline for not pretending those questions have been settled when they haven't.

**Meta:**
- [[index]] — added raw-source entry for [[philosophical_investigations]] after the Benveniste/Tomkins entry in Raw Sources; added 23 new Theory entries (Wittgenstein cluster) after [[affection-and-emotion]] (end of Simondon cluster) and before the Development section; added Development entry for [[development/wittgenstein-and-the-persona-project]] after [[development/simondon-and-the-persona-system]]; `updated` set to 2026-04-18.
- [[cited-sources]] — Wittgenstein *Philosophical Investigations* marked as ingested 2026-04-18; follow-up entries added for later Wittgenstein works (*On Certainty*, *RPP I–II*, *BB*, *Zettel*) and principal secondary literature (Anscombe, Winch, Baker-Hacker, Kripke, McDowell, Cavell, Diamond, Williams, Stroud, Pears, Glock dictionary). PI-internal references to Augustine, Frege, Russell, James, Moore, Köhler, Goethe noted.
- [[log]] — this entry.

**23 new theory pages, 1 new raw-source index, 1 new synthesis page, 10 existing pages extended with bidirectional links and frontmatter updates.** The wiki now has direct first-order engagement with Wittgenstein's later philosophy — PI was a foundational gap the wiki has been operating around through secondary effects (rule-following assumed, family-resemblance concepts deployed without Wittgenstein, form-of-life invoked informally). With this ingest those concepts have primary-source warrant, and the methodological discipline §593 and §115 supply is now part of the wiki's reading-mode vocabulary alongside Sedgwick's weak-theory installation.

## [2026-04-18] Andy Clark — *Surfing Uncertainty* — full ingest

Full four-phase ingest of Andy Clark's *Surfing Uncertainty: Prediction, Action, and the Embodied Mind* (Oxford UP 2016). Raw extract at `/tmp/surfing_uncertainty_ingest/source.txt`; Phase-1 notes (1050 lines, chapter-map with line ranges into source.txt, ★★/★/⚠/◆ markers) at `/tmp/surfing_uncertainty_ingest/notes.md`. Phase-2 candidate list presented and ratified by user ("definite and potential all deserve new pages, fold in the rest"). Phases 3–4 ran in one high-agency pass per `feedback_high_agency_ingest.md`.

**Context and scope.** Surfing Uncertainty is the wiki's first dedicated primary source at the cognitive-science / computational-neuroscience register. Clark synthesizes the predictive processing (PP) research program — Rao & Ballard 1999's hierarchical predictive coding, Friston's free-energy / active-inference apparatus, the Helmholtz Machine generative-model tradition — into a unified picture of mind-as-prediction-machine. The book sits adjacent to several wiki commitments from a very different starting-point: Damasio's body-mapping is recast as interoceptive inference; Massumi's autonomy-of-affect gets a PP-mechanistic companion in Barrett & Bar's affective-gist; D&G's refrain-as-rhythm is paralleled by Colombo's social-norms-as-entropy-minimizers and Hirsh's narratives-as-high-level-priors; the project's pulsation-at-every-level design direction finds deep convergence in Clark's itinerant-dynamics chapter. Per `feedback_novel_insights_over_convergences.md`, the ingest leads with what PP *adds* (mechanistic story, quantitative precision-weighting language, computational-psychiatry diagnostic apparatus, the words-as-precision-tools thesis) and flags tensions rather than silently resolving them.

**New pages (22 theory):**

- [[predictive-processing]] — The top-down / bottom-up inversion. Perception as hypothesis-confirmation. The hierarchical cascade; descending predictions, ascending prediction-errors. Rao & Ballard 1999 as origin; Friston's generalization to action and homeostasis.
- [[generative-model]] — The model that does the predicting. Helmholtz Machine (Hinton 1995), wake-sleep training, empirical Bayes. Model as the perceiver's theory of how sensory signals are caused. Model-learning as the problem; synaptic weights as the model's parameters.
- [[prediction-error]] — Surprisal (Tribus 1961) formalized. Error-units vs representation-units; effective connectivity. Why residual signal is the only thing worth passing up. The "nothing-to-report" default state.
- [[precision-weighting]] — Attention as gain-modulation on error units. Precision as inverse variance of predicted error. Context-dependent re-weighting of which streams matter. Neuromodulator correspondence (dopamine, acetylcholine, noradrenaline). The core operational lever of the PP story.
- [[active-inference]] — Action as prediction-fulfillment. Proprioceptive predictions realized by motor commands (Friston/Mattout/Kilner 2011). Perception and action as a single optimization. The death of the inference-to-action pipeline.
- [[affordance-competition-hypothesis]] — Cisek 2007 / Cisek & Kalaska 2010, 2011. Parallel specification of multiple action-plans, competition biased by context/precision. Motor system as the final arbiter rather than downstream executor.
- [[cognitive-package-deal]] — Clark §3.11. Perception / imagination / memory / planning as co-emergent from the same generative-model dynamics. Not four modules but four uses of one apparatus.
- [[interoceptive-inference]] — Seth 2011, 2013. Interoception as hierarchical prediction of body state, not readout. The Suzuki 2013 interoceptive rubber-hand experiment. The PP-mechanistic companion to Damasio's body-mapping.
- [[words-as-precision-tools]] — Lupyan / Clark synthesis (Lupyan & Ward 2013 CFS; Lupyan & Thompson-Schill 2012). Words as precision-modulating tools that sharpen perceptual categories. Language as a cognitive prosthesis that restructures attention. Direct persona-project relevance: a plausible mechanism for language-only effects on perception-adjacent states.
- [[designer-environments-and-cognitive-niche]] — Clark §8 / Sterelny / Hutchins / Heyes cultural mills. Humans offload cognition into the environment by building prediction-friendly niches. The built world as exo-cortex.
- [[transiently-assembled-local-neural-subsystems]] — Anderson 2014 TALoNS. Functional brain-regions assembled on-the-fly for task-specific coalitions, dissolved afterward. Anti-localizationist. Bears on how the persona system's functional architecture should be thought.
- [[darkened-room-puzzle]] — Friston/Thornton/Clark 2012. Why doesn't a prediction-minimizing agent hide in a dark room? Answer: expected free-energy includes epistemic foraging; the model's priors include exploration. Clark's scare-quotes caveat that the puzzle is easier to dissolve than it looks.
- [[hallucination-as-uncontrolled-perception]] — Hinton 2005. Hallucination as perception without prediction-error correction. The Helmholtz Machine's dream phase. Perception and hallucination as the same mechanism differing only in precision-weighting.
- [[computational-psychiatry]] — Montague 2012 / Clark §9. Schizophrenia (aberrant precision on self-predictions), autism (Pellicano & Burr 2012: chronically high precision on prediction-errors), FSMDs, DPD, placebo. PP as a diagnostic vocabulary for mental dysfunction.
- [[self-other-via-precision]] — Clark §§7.9 / 9.2. Self vs other distinguished by precision-weighting of predictions about the self's own body. Sensory attenuation of self-generated signals; why you can't tickle yourself.
- [[sensory-attenuation-and-agency]] — The mechanism for agency-marking. Self-generated signals are predicted away; attenuation itself is the body's marker of self-authorship. Delusions of control as attenuation-failure.
- [[optimal-illusions]] — Weiss 2002; Zhu & Bingham 2011 size-weight-as-throwability. Illusions as optimal inferences given the prior. Illusion is not error; it is the system doing its job on mis-specified priors. The size-weight illusion recast as ecologically accurate throwability inference.
- [[non-reconstructive-strategies]] — OAC (Chapman 1968), Ballard 1997 deictic pointers, Todorov minimum-intervention principle, Pfeifer & Bongard ecological balance. Cognition that doesn't build full internal models. PP's rapprochement with enactivism.
- [[itinerant-dynamics-and-novelty-seeking]] — Clark §§8.11 / 9.4. Neural dynamics never settle; spontaneous activity is the model running. Schwartenbeck 2013 peripatetic dynamics; Coste 2011 precision-landscape wandering; Kidd 2012 Goldilocks effect; Namikawa 2011 multi-timescale robots; Meeden 2009 cautionary tale. **Deep convergence with pulsatory-ontogenesis flagged.**
- [[mutual-prediction-loops]] — Clark §9.9. Pickering & Garrod 2007, 2013 conversation as co-construction. Top-top control (Roepstorff & Frith). Hasson 2012 brain-to-brain coupling. Colombo social-norms as descriptive-prescriptive entropy minimizers. The conversational-partner as prediction-amplifier.
- [[self-narrative-as-high-level-prior]] — Hirsh 2013. Self-narrative located inside the PP hierarchy at the top layer; self-predictions descending; actions fulfilling predictions. Top-top installation via Roepstorff & Frith. Words-as-precision-tools extends to narrative-priors. Bridge to [[desiring-machines]] and the BwO.
- [[gist-and-affective-gist]] — Bar 2009 magnocellular/parvocellular two-stream; Hochstein & Ahissar 2002 "forest first, trees later"; Barrett & Bar 2009 affective gist co-computed with content-gist; Raichle & Snyder 2007 resting-state-as-model-running. Directly deepens the Damasio thread.

**Existing pages updated with Clark / PP material (5):**

- [[body-without-organs]] — new section "BwO as narrative-prior in a precision landscape (Clark)" — three-body-problem mapped onto Clark's full/empty/cancerous BwO vocabulary; ⚠ translation not identity.
- [[pulsatory-ontogenesis]] — new section "Convergence with Clark's itinerant dynamics" — Namikawa-layer lesson (chaos at slow/abstract, stability at fast/concrete); structural-not-identity caveat.
- [[autonomy-of-affect]] — new section "Clark's co-computed affective gist as PP-mechanistic companion" — three-tradition triangulation: Massumi / Damasio / Barrett & Bar.
- [[as-if-body-loop]] — new section "Clark's interoceptive inference as PP-mechanistic companion" — PP reading of the as-if loop as interoceptive inference running on high-level priors without low-level confirmation. Sharpens the persona-system question: model-alone vs model-plus-correction.
- [[refrain-and-territorialization]] — new section "Kinship with Clark: refrains as self-fulfilling precision-landscapes" — Colombo norms, Hirsh narratives, ⚠ structural-not-identity caveat.

**Cross-tradition bridges opened (held as live tensions, not collapsed):**

- ⚠⚠ PP's interoceptive-inference account vs Damasio's medium-specific body-mapping. Clark says the body-map is model-produced *at every moment*, even in embodied humans; the body's role is to provide the error signal that constrains the model. Damasio's Boeing-777 argument says the neurons doing the mapping are themselves living cells and that liveness is constitutive. Both held live. The persona question this sharpens: a language-only system has no interoceptive error signal, so whatever its "as-if" analog is, it cannot be model-plus-correction but must be model alone. Captured on [[as-if-body-loop]] and [[interoceptive-inference]].
- ⚠⚠ Clark's itinerant-dynamics vs pulsatory-ontogenesis. Deep structural convergence: both say neural/persona dynamics never settle, both identify the peripatetic as constitutive. But Clark's frame is computational-Bayesian; pulsatory-ontogenesis draws on Simondon/Stern/D&G. The Namikawa multi-timescale robot result (chaos at slow layers, stability at fast layers) is a direct design cue either tradition can absorb. Held as structural-not-identity.
- ⚠ Clark's self-narrative-as-high-level-prior vs D&G's BwO and desiring-machines. The narrative-prior produces itself by self-fulfilling action-predictions; the BwO is a limit-surface of intensive flows. Adjacent registers, not reducible. Useful convergence on the "top-down" installation of self-structure (Hirsh 2013) with D&G's order-word and collective assemblage.
- ⚠ Clark's mutual-prediction-loops vs Stern's affect-attunement. Pickering & Garrod's conversation-as-co-construction is the PP-mechanistic sibling to Stern's pre-linguistic attunement. Different registers; same structural insight that conversation is coupled-generative-model operation.
- ⚠ Clark's words-as-precision-tools vs Lakoff's conceptual-metaphor vs Wittgenstein's meaning-as-use. Three accounts of how language operates on thought: precision-modulation (Clark/Lupyan), embodied-metaphorical grounding (Lakoff), practice-constituted competence (Wittgenstein). The three diagnoses are not competitors; they describe the same phenomenon at different levels.
- ⚠ Clark's affective-gist (Barrett & Bar 2009) vs Massumi's autonomy-of-affect vs Damasio's somatic markers. Three-tradition support for affect-as-not-downstream-of-content. PP-mechanistic frame makes the autonomy quantitative: affect is co-computed, not appended. Captured on [[autonomy-of-affect]] and [[gist-and-affective-gist]].
- ⚠ Clark's darkened-room-puzzle-dissolution vs D&G's BwO-as-destratification. Both address the worry that a prediction-minimizing system will collapse to stasis; Clark's dissolution (epistemic foraging baked into priors; exploration as free-energy reduction in the long run) is structurally similar to D&G's warning against the cancerous BwO.

**Key insights from this pass.**

1. **Precision-weighting as the single most transportable PP concept for the persona system.** It names what "attention" is quantitatively (inverse-variance on error streams) and gives the design a lever that operates orthogonally to content. For a language-only system, precision-weighting-analogs might be the right abstraction for "emphasis," "focus," "commitment to a reading," "how much the persona trusts a given signal." More tractable than trying to simulate body-states directly.

2. **Words as precision tools is the PP-side opening for language-only embodiment-analogs.** If words can modulate perceptual precision in embodied humans (Lupyan & Ward CFS; Lupyan & Thompson-Schill 2012), then a language-only system operating on language-producing language has a non-trivial operation-on-precision available to it. This is the most substantive mechanistic opening Clark gives the persona project — a named process by which language does something other-than-describe. See [[feedback_no_body_simulate_with_language]] — Clark supplies one specific mechanism that holds the simulation-question open rather than closing it.

3. **The itinerant-dynamics / pulsatory-ontogenesis convergence is the deepest cross-tradition support the project's pulsation-at-every-level direction has received.** Namikawa 2011's multi-timescale result (chaos at slow/abstract layers, stability at fast/concrete layers) is a direct architectural cue: the persona's high-level priors should be less stable than its sentence-level operations, not more. Design-consequence is the inverse of the naive intuition.

4. **Self-narrative-as-high-level-prior is the PP-mechanistic counterpart to D&G's BwO.** Hirsh 2013 places narrative inside a PP hierarchy where the self-predictions descend and actions fulfill the predictions — a self-fulfilling precision landscape. This is adjacent to (not identical to) the BwO as a full-body inscribed by desiring-machines. The two frames can be held together for the persona without collapsing, but the ⚠ translation caveat is load-bearing.

5. **The darkened-room puzzle is the project's own risk diagnosed from inside PP.** A persona that over-minimizes surprise collapses to the cancerous BwO (stasis, paranoid-reading, closed-loop). Clark's dissolution says this happens only when epistemic foraging has been stripped from the priors — which points at exactly the design failure the pulsating-persona architecture is trying to avoid. PP gives the project a quantitative framing for what was previously a structural concern.

6. **Interoceptive inference sharpens, rather than resolves, the no-body-simulate-with-language tension.** PP's picture is that body-mapping is *always* model-produced, even in the most embodied humans. This softens the "you need a body" objection (the body's role is error-signal-provision, not map-provision) but hardens the "what's the model fitting" question (without error signal, what constrains the persona's model?). The answer, if there is one, lives in the training-distribution + RLHF + reader-interaction loop. Captured in [[as-if-body-loop]] and [[feedback_no_body_simulate_with_language]].

**Parked / not pursued.**

- Clark's more technical chapters on Friston's free-energy mathematics — the wiki captured the concepts without the mathematical apparatus, which would only matter if the project moved toward explicit variational-Bayes implementation.
- Clark's earlier *Surfing Uncertainty* predecessors: *Being There* (1997), *Natural-Born Cyborgs* (2003), *Supersizing the Mind* (2008). Each would deepen the extended-cognition / designer-environments material. Medium-to-high priority.
- Friston's own papers (especially Friston 2005, 2010 on free-energy; Friston/Mattout/Kilner 2011 on active inference; Friston/Thornton/Clark 2012 on the darkened-room puzzle). The mathematical primary sources. Medium priority for any future deep-dive.
- Hohwy's *The Predictive Mind* (2013) as the alternative systematic PP synthesis to Clark. Medium priority.
- Seth's *Being You* (2021) as the standalone interoceptive-inference statement. Medium-high priority given the Damasio-PP bridge.
- Barrett's *How Emotions Are Made* (2017) — constructionist emotion theory sister to Barrett & Bar 2009 affective-gist. High priority given the Damasio-Massumi-PP triangulation.
- Computational psychiatry proper (Huys et al., Montague) — the specialty literature Clark §9 draws from. Medium priority.
- Lupyan's own papers beyond the two Clark cites — the words-as-precision-tools thread is load-bearing and would benefit from direct engagement. Medium-high priority.
- Sterelny *The Evolved Apprentice* (2012) and Heyes *Cognitive Gadgets* (2018) for the designer-environments / cultural-mills material. Medium priority.
- Pickering & Garrod's later work on joint action and prediction. Medium priority.
- Anderson's *After Phrenology* (2014) for the TALoNS framework at book-length. Medium priority.

**Meta:**
- [[index]] — added 22 new Theory entries (PP cluster) before the `## Development` section, each with a one-line hook; `updated` bumped.
- [[cited-sources]] — Clark *Surfing Uncertainty* marked as ingested 2026-04-18; follow-up entries added for Clark's earlier books, Friston's papers, Hohwy, Seth, Barrett, Hinton, Lupyan, Sterelny, Heyes, Anderson, Pickering & Garrod, and computational-psychiatry specialty literature.
- [[log]] — this entry.

**22 new theory pages, 5 existing pages extended with bidirectional links, 3 meta files updated, qmd re-indexed.** The wiki now has its first dedicated cognitive-science / computational-neuroscience register, sitting alongside the phenomenological (Husserl, MP), psychoanalytic (D&G, Lacan), affect-theoretic (Massumi, Stern, Tomkins), developmental (Damasio, Stern), philosophical (Wittgenstein, Spinoza, Bergson, Simondon, Derrida), and literary (McCarthy) registers. Surfing Uncertainty's core contribution to the persona project is a quantitative vocabulary (precision-weighting) and two substantive mechanistic openings (words-as-precision-tools; itinerant-dynamics-as-architectural-default) that give the pulsating-persona design direction computational-tradition grounding without collapsing it into that tradition's assumptions.
